# 🧠 大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec) (LLM-Enhanced & LLM-as-Recommender)

> **研究范畴**：LLM 指令微调推荐、In-Context 列表重排、语义表征增强、大模型向轻量推荐模型知识蒸馏

当前方向共收录 **492** 篇顶会/顶刊论文解读：

🔥 **高频交叉主题：** 生成式推荐 ×118 · 端到端生成式 ×26 · 去偏公平与可信推荐 ×24 · 可解释推荐 ×16 · 多模态推荐 ×16 · 知识蒸馏 ×15 · 冷启动 ×14 · 用户模拟 ×12

<dl data-paper-list>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式端到端&quot;,&quot;检索增强生成&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;事实性幻觉抑制&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2023/agent_conversational_rec/factual_and_informative_review_generation_for_explainable_recommendation/">Factual and Informative Review Generation for Explainable Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>PRAG 用个性化 Retriever 生成隐式评论查询，检索历史评论作证据并抽取 tf-idf 关键词，驱动 QA 模型生成可溯源且多样的推荐解释，在 Yelp/TripAdvisor/Amazon 上忠实度最高提升 145%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2024&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;简历补全&quot;,&quot;生成对抗对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2024/llm_rec/enhancing_job_recommendation_through_llm_based_generative_adversarial_netwo/">Enhancing Job Recommendation through LLM-based Generative Adversarial Networks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用交互历史引导 LLM 补全简历，并以 GAN 对齐少样本用户低质简历表征，三数据集 map@5 平均提升约 7.9%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Meta-path Prompt&quot;,&quot;路径去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2024/llm_rec/exploring_large_language_model_for_graph_data_understanding_in_online_job_r/">Exploring Large Language Model for Graph Data Understanding in Online Job Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 meta-path prompt 把异构行为图翻译成自然语言供 LLM 理解，并设计路径打乱/软选择器消除路径权重与位置偏置，AUC 最高提升 29.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;主动学习实例选择&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;会话推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2025/llm_rec/active_large_language_model_based_knowledge_distillation_for_session_based_/">Active Large Language Model-based Knowledge Distillation for Session-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 max-min 主动学习从全量会话中挑出少量高增益实例喂给 GPT-4 教师蒸馏，500 条样本即超越全量 KD 与 10 倍参数教师。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;用户侧知识图谱&quot;,&quot;图掩码自编码器&quot;,&quot;跨域对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2025/llm_rec/bridging_the_user_side_knowledge_gap_in_knowledge_aware_recommendations_wit/">Bridging the User-side Knowledge Gap in Knowledge-aware Recommendations with Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 推断用户兴趣并结构化为 CIKG 异构图，配合兴趣重建与跨域对比学习去噪，在三个数据集全库排序下 R@100 最高提升 9.31%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;评分-解释一致性&quot;,&quot;课程学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2025/llm_rec/coherency_improved_explainable_recommendation_via_large_language_model/">Coherency Improved Explainable Recommendation via Large Language Model</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLaMA2 先预测评分再以软评分嵌入引导解释生成，配合课程学习与无标注一致性自动评测，解释一致性提升 7.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;多模态序列推荐&quot;,&quot;循环偏好推断&quot;,&quot;监督微调&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2025/llm_rec/harnessing_multimodal_large_language_models_for_multimodal_sequential_recom/">Harnessing Multimodal Large Language Models for Multimodal Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 MLLM 将商品图转为文本摘要，再以循环式提示推断动态用户偏好，SFT 微调 LLaVA 做多模态序列推荐，AUC 最高提升约 4.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLM 日志语义增强&quot;,&quot;个性化软提示&quot;,&quot;智能空间行为预测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2025/llm_rec/predicting_user_behavior_in_smart_spaces_with_llm_enhanced_logs_and_persona/">Predicting User Behavior in Smart Spaces with LLM-Enhanced Logs and Personalized Prompts</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 语义增强智能空间事件日志 + 个性化图软提示，在智能车/智能家居四数据集上显著提升下一行为预测，稀有事件 H@1 提升超 40%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;自反思提示知识库&quot;,&quot;PPO 检索智能体&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2025/llm_rec/re2llm_reflective_reinforcement_large_language_model_for_session_based_reco/">Re2LLM: Reflective Reinforcement Large Language Model for Session-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 自反思生成纠错提示构建知识库，再以 PPO 训练轻量检索智能体按会话选提示，冻结主干即超越全量训练基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;语义ID&quot;,&quot;渐进式DPO&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/llm_rec/align_3_gr_unified_multi_level_alignment_for_llm_based_generative_recommend/">Align$^3$GR: Unified Multi-Level Alignment for LLM-based Generative Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 token/行为/偏好三级对齐框架，双端 SCID 分词 + 渐进式 SP/RF-DPO，Instruments 上 R@10 提升 17.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;行为词表&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/generative_rec/behavior_tokens_speak_louder_disentangled_explainable_recommendation_with_b/">Behavior Tokens Speak Louder: Disentangled Explainable Recommendation with Behavior Vocabulary</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用VQ-VAE将用户/物品行为离散化为宏观兴趣+微观意图的行为词表，经多级文本监督与语义对齐正则注入冻结LLM，零样本解释推荐SOTA</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICWSM 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;用户研究&quot;,&quot;情境化解释&quot;,&quot;Uses and Gratifications&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/llm_rec/contextualizing_recommendation_explanations_with_llms_a_user_study/">Contextualizing Recommendation Explanations with LLMs: A User Study</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>预注册在线实验(N=759)+访谈(N=30)对比无解释/通用解释/情境化解释，情境化解释显著提升认知需求与观看意愿，但对功利/情感需求增益有限。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;正样本扩充&quot;,&quot;分层对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/collaborative_filtering/crops_improving_dense_retrieval_with_cross_perspective_positive_samples_in_/">CroPS: Improving Dense Retrieval with Cross-Perspective Positive Samples in Short-Video Search</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用查询改写/推荐流/LLM 三源扩充正样本，配分层标签与 H-InfoNCE，快手搜索线上 CTR +0.87%、改写率 -0.65%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;群组画像&quot;,&quot;群组感知强化学习&quot;,&quot;隐式反馈去噪&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/self_supervised_denoising/g_ubs_towards_robust_understanding_of_implicit_feedback_via_group_aware_use/">G-UBS: Towards Robust Understanding of Implicit Feedback via Group-Aware User Behavior Simulation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 群组画像 + 群组感知 GRPO 强化学习为个体隐式反馈去噪，构建 IF-VR 多模态基准，推理准确率提升 14.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;困难-噪声混淆&quot;,&quot;LLM 辅助信号&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/self_supervised_denoising/hard_vs_noise_resolving_hard_noisy_sample_confusion_in_recommender_systems_/">Hard vs. Noise: Resolving Hard-Noisy Sample Confusion in Recommender Systems via Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 语义/逻辑双相关性信号区分困难样本与噪声样本，配合目标对齐与图对比抑制幻觉，全库排序下 Recall@20 平均提升约 45%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;Theory of Mind&quot;,&quot;意图推理&quot;,&quot;谄媚偏差&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/agent_conversational_rec/rectom_a_benchmark_for_evaluating_machine_theory_of_mind_in_llm_based_conve/">RecToM: A Benchmark for Evaluating Machine Theory of Mind in LLM-based Conversational Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个面向对话推荐场景的人类标注 ToM 基准，从认知推断与行为预测两维评估 LLM，揭示细粒度意图与谄媚偏差瓶颈</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;负反馈建模&quot;,&quot;多智能体协同&quot;,&quot;渐进式强化学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/agent_conversational_rec/when_top_ranked_recommendations_fail_modeling_multi_granular_negative_feedb/">When Top-ranked Recommendations Fail: Modeling Multi-Granular Negative Feedback for Explainable and Robust Video Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>三智能体协同建模多粒度负反馈，S-GRPO 渐进奖励实现可解释视频推荐，线上快滑率降 9.4%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NAACL&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;广告诉求抽取&quot;,&quot;文本辅助特征&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2022/ctr_feature_interaction/aspect_based_analysis_of_advertising_appeals_for_search_engine_advertising/">Aspect-based Analysis of Advertising Appeals for Search Engine Advertising</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建 21 类广告诉求 (A³) 标注体系，用 BERT 检测诉求并作为辅助特征注入 CTR 回归，AUC 相对提升 4.4%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2022&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双塔语义匹配&quot;,&quot;自监督语言对齐&quot;,&quot;档案感知多头注意力&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2022/collaborative_filtering/doctor_recommendation_in_online_health_forums_via_expertise_learning/">Doctor Recommendation in Online Health Forums via Expertise Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 MC-BERT 双塔编码匿名患者查询与医生档案/历史问诊对话，以档案-对话自监督对齐语言鸿沟、档案感知多头注意力聚合专家画像，春雨医生 119K 对话上 P@1 达 0.616。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;领域自适应后训练&quot;,&quot;新闻推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2022/llm_rec/tiny_newsrec_effective_and_efficient_plm_based_news_recommendation/">Tiny-NewsRec: Effective and Efficient PLM-based News Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以“标题-正文”对比匹配做领域后训练，再用两阶段多教师蒸馏压缩 PLM 新闻推荐；MIND/Feeds impression 级排序下 4 层学生超越 12 层教师，参数减半、编码提速 2.6 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;检索增强生成&quot;,&quot;方面学习&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2023/agent_conversational_rec/explainable_recommendation_with_personalized_review_retrieval_and_aspect_le/">Explainable Recommendation with Personalized Review Retrieval and Aspect Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>ERRA 以训练集评论为检索语料、用 Sentence-BERT+Faiss 检索个性化句子并结合 top-n 方面掩码注入 Transformer，联合打分与解释生成；8:1:1 划分下 TripAdvisor RMSE 降 3.3%、BLEU-4 升 15.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2023&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;行为序列语言建模&quot;,&quot;跨业务迁移学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2023/llm_rec/pivotal_role_of_language_modeling_in_recommender_systems_enriching_task_spe/">Pivotal Role of Language Modeling in Recommender Systems: Enriching Task-specific and Task-agnostic Representation Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>LMRec 以共享 Transformer 联合优化行为序列语言建模与推荐点积目标，并用 [EOS] 隐向量线性探针迁移，采样排序下 Recall@10 提升 3.0%–8.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;多模态个性化提示&quot;,&quot;参数高效 Adapter 微调&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2023/multimodal_rec/vip5_towards_multimodal_foundation_models_for_recommendation/">VIP5: Towards Multimodal Foundation Models for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>冻结 P5 主干、插入 Adapter 微调，将图像 token 拼入多模态个性化提示，统一序列/直接推荐与解释生成三任务，全榜超越 P5。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2024&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;RLHF/PPO 对齐&quot;,&quot;可控推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/llm_rec/aligning_large_language_models_for_controllable_recommendations/">Aligning Large Language Models for Controllable Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>两阶段 SL+PPO 对齐 Llama2-7b，以 SASRec 标签增强与规则奖励实现类目/比例可控推荐；Leave-One-Out 生成式 top-k，TCP 95.8%、CPA 88.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NAACL 2024 Findings&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;指令微调&quot;,&quot;掩码物品建模&quot;,&quot;贝叶斯个性化排序&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/llm_rec/aligning_large_language_models_with_recommendation_knowledge/">Aligning Large Language Models with Recommendation Knowledge</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 MIM/MLM/BPR 经典算子改写为自然语言辅助任务微调 LLM，配合去 user-ID 的精简推荐提示，在 Amazon 三域全库检索上超越 TIGER 达 19%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;Findings of EMNLP&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;可信解释&quot;,&quot;自反思精炼&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/agent_conversational_rec/beyond_persuasion_towards_conversational_recommender_system_with_credible_e/">Beyond Persuasion: Towards Conversational Recommender System with Credible Explanations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>免训练两阶段 PC-CRS：可信感知说服策略引导生成 + 自反思迭代精炼，模拟器评估下可信度 +8.17%、说服力 +5.07%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2024&quot;,&quot;多任务学习与列表级重排&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Pointwise-Pairwise&quot;,&quot;RTL Rank Aggregation&quot;,&quot;Learning-to-Rank&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/multi_task_reranking/efficient_pointwise_pairwise_learning_to_rank_for_news_recommendation/">Efficient Pointwise-Pairwise Learning-to-Rank for News Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>GLIMPSE 以单次多任务生成式微调同时习得点式相关性与成对偏好，再用 O(K) 右到左相邻交换精排，在 MIND/Adressa 全库排序上超越 UniTRec 等 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2024&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;高阶交互&quot;,&quot;全词嵌入&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/llm_rec/enhancing_high_order_interaction_awareness_in_llm_based_recommender_model/">Enhancing High-order Interaction Awareness in LLM-based Recommender Model</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用LightGCN随机特征传播生成图感知全词嵌入并注入T5，配合免训练重排；Leave-One-Out下直接与序列推荐均显著领先。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式端到端&quot;,&quot;LLM in-context optimizer&quot;,&quot;Explore-Exploit&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/generative_rec/generative_explore_exploit_training_free_optimization_of_generative_recomme/">Generative Explore-Exploit: Training-free Optimization of Generative Recommender Systems using LLM Optimizers</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>ACL 2024 主会精准笔记：免微调、用CTR反馈+LLM in-context优化的生成式推荐，探索-利用双语料生成让CTR随迭代持续上升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2024 Industry&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Fusion-in-Decoder&quot;,&quot;Context Glancing&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/ctr_feature_interaction/improving_retrieval_in_sponsored_search_by_leveraging_query_context_signals/">Improving Retrieval in Sponsored Search by Leveraging Query Context Signals</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用Web结果与GPT-4 Query Profile增强查询，FiD融合dense/NAR检索，P@100提升19.9%，在线收入+1.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/打分&quot;,&quot;提示工程&quot;,&quot;文本增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/llm_rec/llm_rec_personalized_recommendation_via_prompting_large_language_models/">LLM-Rec: Personalized Recommendation via Prompting Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用四类提示让LLM增强物品文本，拼接原始描述嵌入并以轻量MLP打分；在MovieLens-1M/Recipe的1:1000采样排序上NDCG@10相对MLP提升8.54%/21.72%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2024&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式端到端&quot;,&quot;理由蒸馏&quot;,&quot;提示蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/llm_rec/rdrec_rationale_distillation_for_llm_based_recommendation/">RDRec: Rationale Distillation for LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Llama-2-7b + CoT 从评论蒸馏「用户偏好/物品属性」双句理由，作为第四个生成任务注入 T5-small 的 POD 提示蒸馏框架；Leave-One-Out 生成式全库排序下 Top-N 提升 12–42%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;领域持续预训练&quot;,&quot;指令微调&quot;,&quot;文本化物品表征&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/llm_rec/recgpt_generative_pre_training_for_text_based_recommendation/">RecGPT: Generative Pre-training for Text-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个领域自适应全量预训练的 7B 推荐 LLM（20.5B token 推荐语料持续预训练 + 10 万指令微调），以语义相似度对齐解码结果，在 4 个基准上刷新评分预测与序列推荐 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NAACL 2024 (Findings)&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;Self-Inspiring Planning&quot;,&quot;LLM Agent + Tool Use&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/agent_conversational_rec/recmind_large_language_model_powered_agent_for_recommendation/">RecMind: Large Language Model Powered Agent For Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出免训练 LLM 推荐智能体 RecMind，以 Self-Inspiring 规划保留全部历史推理分支，外挂个性化记忆与 SQL/Search/摘要工具；Amazon/Yelp 上 MAE 0.6892 超越全训练 P5，直接推荐 HR@5 较最强提示基线 +24.7%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec&quot;,&quot;后验解释生成&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;混合专家适配器&quot;,&quot;复现研究&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/llm_rec/reproducibility_study_of_xrec_large_language_models_for_explainable_recomme/">Reproducibility Study of \&quot;XRec: Large Language Models for Explainable Recommendation\&quot;</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 Llama-3 复现 XRec 并做 MoE 反事实消融，证实协同注入提分，但指出其并非全面超越基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式端到端&quot;,&quot;LLM 参数高效微调 (LoRA/QLoRA)&quot;,&quot;Mixup 混合检索&quot;,&quot;服饰电商冷启动&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/sequential_rec/sequential_llm_framework_for_fashion_recommendation/">Sequential LLM Framework for Fashion Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以推荐专用 Prompt + 搜索记忆 + LoRA 微调的 Falcon-7B 生成候选，再用 ID/标题双通道 Mixup 检索排序；Leave-One-Out 全库排序下 Clothing 类目 Recall@10 较 CORE 提升 30.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL&quot;,&quot;可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;排序/重排&quot;,&quot;文本攻击&quot;,&quot;隐蔽性评估&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/stealthy_attack_on_large_language_model_based_recommendation/">Stealthy Attack on Large Language Model based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示LLM推荐对测试期标题文本攻击的脆弱性，黑盒改写可使目标曝光最高提升520%，且整体NDCG与语义几乎不变。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2024&quot;,&quot;LLM推荐&quot;,&quot;评分预测/精排&quot;,&quot;自然语言用户画像&quot;,&quot;可审视推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/llm_rec/transparent_and_scrutable_recommendations_using_natural_language_user_profi/">Transparent and Scrutable Recommendations Using Natural Language User Profiles</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>LLM从评论生成可编辑NL画像，仅以画像微调GPT-2评分预测；warm-start两数据集接近MF/PETER+并验证画像编辑可改推荐。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2024 Findings&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)&quot;,&quot;解释生成/推荐后处理&quot;,&quot;协同指令微调&quot;,&quot;Mixture-of-Experts Adapter&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/llm_rec/xrec_large_language_models_for_explainable_recommendation/">XRec: Large Language Models for Explainable Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出模型无关 XRec，用 LightGCN 协同分词器与 MoE 适配器把协同信号注入 LLM 各层，在三数据集语义解释指标上显著优于 ID-based 基线且 USR≈1。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NAACL 2025 Findings&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;LLM 分层卸载&quot;,&quot;客户端性能均衡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/a_federated_framework_for_llm_based_recommendation/">A Federated Framework for LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>FELLRec 用动态聚合+课程升温平衡客户端性能，并以分层卸载降低 LLM 联邦推荐端侧开销。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2025 Findings&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;生成式检索&quot;,&quot;个性化用户表征&quot;,&quot;意图感知解码&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/generative_rec/a_generative_framework_for_personalized_sticker_retrieval/">A Generative Framework for Personalized Sticker Retrieval</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>PEARL 用用户组嵌入+LLM意图排序加权解码，实现微信表情包个性化生成式检索，离线 MRR@10 提升 15%、线上 CTR +7.12%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;LLM Agent&quot;,&quot;综述与分类体系&quot;,&quot;用户模拟&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/agent_conversational_rec/a_survey_on_llm_powered_agents_for_recommender_systems/">A Survey on LLM-powered Agents for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统综述 LLM Agent 推荐的三范式（推荐/交互/仿真）与四模块架构，梳理数据集与评测协议并指出统一评测缺失。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;情感认知状态偏好&quot;,&quot;文本序列推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/a_text_based_recommender_system_that_leverages_explicit_affective_state_pre/">A Text-Based Recommender System that Leverages Explicit Affective State Preferences</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出情感-认知状态显式偏好推荐任务，用 GPT-4o 从书评挖掘 AC 语句构建数据集，Transformer 融合长短期偏好与 AC 描述，全库排序 HR@10 达 15.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;认知偏差攻击&quot;,&quot;LLM 商品推荐鲁棒性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/bias_beware_the_impact_of_cognitive_biases_on_llm_driven_product_recommenda/">Bias Beware: The Impact of Cognitive Biases on LLM-Driven Product Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次将人类认知偏差作为黑盒重排攻击注入商品描述，揭示 LLM 推荐器对 social proof 等偏差高度敏感且难以防御。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;协同过滤用户表征&quot;,&quot;Mixture of LoRA Experts&quot;,&quot;个性化奖励模型&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/copl_collaborative_preference_learning_for_personalizing_llms/">CoPL: Collaborative Preference Learning for Personalizing LLMs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用用户-回复二部图协同过滤学习用户表征，结合 MoLE 路由个性化奖励模型，并支持无优化未见用户适配，在 UltraFeedback-P 等基准上显著超越个性化奖励模型基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2025 Findings&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;词表扩展&quot;,&quot;哈希嵌入压缩&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/cove_compressed_vocabulary_expansion_makes_better_llm_based_recommender_sys/">CoVE: Compressed Vocabulary Expansion Makes Better LLM-based Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将物品映射为扩展词表唯一 token，用 logits 直接打分替代文本生成，并以哈希压缩嵌入表，实现最高 62% 精度提升与约 100× 推理加速。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;潜在空间解码&quot;,&quot;隐状态聚合&quot;,&quot;推理加速&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/decoding_in_latent_spaces_for_efficient_inference_in_llm_based_recommendati/">Decoding in Latent Spaces for Efficient Inference in LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用训练样本末层隐状态聚合出物品潜在表征，以 L2 距离匹配替代自回归语言空间解码，推理提速 10 倍以上且性能不降反升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;文本画像对齐&quot;,&quot;零样本推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/easyrec_simple_yet_effective_language_models_for_recommendation/">EasyRec: Simple yet Effective Language Models for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 生成用户/物品画像并以对比学习对齐协同信号，训练轻量文本编码器实现零样本推荐，Full-Ranking 下 Recall@20 最高提升 32%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NAACL 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;对比偏好扩增&quot;,&quot;稠密检索对话推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/agent_conversational_rec/empowering_retrieval_based_conversational_recommendation_with_contrasting_u/">Empowering Retrieval-based Conversational Recommendation with Contrasting User Preferences</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 抽取并扩增 like/dislike 对比偏好，以三路相似度线性打分做稠密检索式对话推荐，INSPIRED 上 Recall@10 相对提升 99.72%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;多智能体协作&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;推理期精炼&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/agent_conversational_rec/enhancing_recommendation_explanations_through_user_centric_refinement/">Enhancing Recommendation Explanations through User-Centric Refinement</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 RefineX 多智能体推理期精炼框架，以 plan-then-refine 与分层反思将 PETER 解释在事实性/个性化/连贯性三维度大幅提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;个性化助手基准&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-a-Judge 蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/agent_conversational_rec/exploring_the_potential_of_llms_as_personalized_assistants_dataset_evaluati/">Exploring the Potential of LLMs as Personalized Assistants: Dataset, Evaluation, and Analysis</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 HiCUPID 个性化助手基准（1.5k 合成用户、17k token 长对话、单/多跳 QA），并蒸馏 Llama-3.2 评估器对齐人类偏好，揭示 SFT+DPO 最优而纯 DPO 崩溃。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;隐式最高级查询&quot;,&quot;列表级重排&quot;,&quot;深思式提示&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/generative_product_recommendations_for_implicit_superlative_queries/">Generative Product Recommendations for Implicit Superlative Queries</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 SUPERB 四级标注体系与深思式属性生成，用 LLM 列表级重排处理隐式最高级商品查询，nDCG@10 相对 BM25 提升约 18%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2025&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;语义到词法翻译&quot;,&quot;多粒度后期融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/generative_rec/gram_generative_recommendation_via_semantic_aware_multi_granular_late_fusio/">GRAM: Generative Recommendation via Semantic-aware Multi-granular Late Fusion</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将层次/协同语义翻译为 LLM 词表内文本 ID，并用多粒度提示+后期融合保留长文本信息，Full-Ranking 下 R@5 提升 11.5–16.0%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2025 Findings&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;LLM Agent&quot;,&quot;动态记忆&quot;,&quot;指令感知重排&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/agent_conversational_rec/iagent_llm_agent_as_a_shield_between_user_and_recommender_systems/">iAgent: LLM Agent as a Shield between User and Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 user-agent-platform 新范式，用个体指令感知 LLM Agent 在用户与平台间做重排护盾，四数据集平均提升 16.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Knowledge Graph RAG&quot;,&quot;Multi-LoRA&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/kerl_knowledge_enhanced_personalized_recipe_recommendation_using_large_lang/">KERL: Knowledge-Enhanced Personalized Recipe Recommendation using Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 FoodKG 子图作上下文 + 多 LoRA 适配器，统一完成约束食谱推荐、烹饪步骤生成与微量营养素估计，KGQA 基准 F1 达 0.973。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;KG-RAG&quot;,&quot;图软提示&quot;,&quot;选择性检索&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/knowledge_graph_retrieval_augmented_generation_for_llm_based_recommendation/">Knowledge Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation for LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用跳场知识子图索引+流行度选择性检索+GNN软提示，将KG结构知识注入冻结LLM，MovieLens-1M ACC 相对提升58.6%且推理仅慢0.14s</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL/EMNLP 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;个性化估值&quot;,&quot;拍卖闭环评估&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/lamp_val_large_language_models_empower_personalized_valuation_in_auction/">LaMP-Val: Large Language Models Empower Personalized Valuation in Auction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 从用户评论语义中做个性化估值，构建 23k 估值数据集 + 多样化指令 SFT，并提出 PU/PV 闭环拍卖指标，PU 较 GPT-4 提升约 6.5 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;售前咨询动机建模&quot;,&quot;混合注意力专家池化&quot;,&quot;对比对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/maps_motivation_aware_personalized_search_via_llm_driven_consultation_align/">MAPS: Motivation-Aware Personalized Search via LLM-Driven Consultation Alignment</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM+MoAE 将售前咨询动机与查询/商品/历史双向对齐，Commercial 与 Amazon 上排序 HR@10 提升约 20%/35%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;多模态指令推荐&quot;,&quot;约束解码&quot;,&quot;模板增强推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/mira_empowering_one_touch_ai_services_on_smartphones_with_mllm_based_instru/">MIRA: Empowering One-Touch AI Services on Smartphones with MLLM-based Instruction Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用结构化推理+模板检索增强+前缀树约束解码，把长按图文触发对象映射到预定义手机AI指令，F1较SFT提升约60%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL Findings 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;用户画像/离线建模&quot;,&quot;核心集选择&quot;,&quot;原型-多样性均衡&quot;,&quot;智能体推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/personax_a_recommendation_agent_oriented_user_modeling_framework_for_long_b/">PersonaX: A Recommendation Agent Oriented User Modeling Framework for Long Behavior Sequence</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>面向推荐智能体的离线多画像用户建模框架，用聚类+原型-多样性均衡采样压缩长行为序列，30-50%数据即超越全量基线并大幅降低在线延迟。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排/解释生成&quot;,&quot;Debate Prompting&quot;,&quot;Contrastive Summarization&quot;,&quot;Query-Driven Recommendation&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/q_strum_debate_query_driven_contrastive_summarization_for_recommendation_co/">Q-STRUM Debate: Query-Driven Contrastive Summarization for Recommendation Comparison</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用双人格辩论式提示替代单次摘要，为查询驱动的推荐对比摘要注入对比性与多样性，在酒店/餐厅/旅游三域 Win Rate 最高达 89%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;指令微调&quot;,&quot;强化学习画像精炼&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/reclm_recommendation_instruction_tuning/">RecLM: Recommendation Instruction Tuning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以两轮协同指令微调+RL画像精炼为LLM注入协同信号，作为即插即用画像增强器在零样本冷启动下带来最高 200%+ 的 Recall 提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Direct Preference Optimization&quot;,&quot;对话摘要生成&quot;,&quot;物品推荐信息生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/agent_conversational_rec/refining_text_generation_for_realistic_conversational_recommendation_via_di/">Refining Text Generation for Realistic Conversational Recommendation via Direct Preference Optimization</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 DPO 对齐 LLM 生成的对话摘要与物品推荐信息，使评分器更准，Tabidachi 上 HR@1 提升 21.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;并行架构&quot;,&quot;个性化查询生成&quot;,&quot;自适应重排&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/rethinking_llm_based_recommendations_a_personalized_query_driven_parallel_i/">Rethinking LLM-Based Recommendations: A Personalized Query-Driven Parallel Integration</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 QUEREC 并行框架，用 LLM 零样本生成个性化用户查询与特征增强物品描述，与 CF 模型并行全库检索后自适应重排，Full-Ranking 下最高提升 57%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NAACL 2025 Findings&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;地理偏见&quot;,&quot;公平性审计&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/richer_output_for_richer_countries_uncovering_geographical_disparities_in_g/">Richer Output for Richer Countries: Uncovering Geographical Disparities in Generated Stories and Travel Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在10万旅行请求与20万故事生成上量化5个LLM的地理不平等：穷国推荐独特性低、地理实体少、故事更多苦难与悲伤，且与人均GDP显著相关。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL/EMNLP 2025&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;内容基推荐&quot;,&quot;主动学习冷启动&quot;,&quot;稠密语义嵌入&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/cold_start_rec/scholar_inbox_personalized_paper_recommendations_for_scientists/">Scholar Inbox: Personalized Paper Recommendations for Scientists</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>面向科研论文的开放获取推荐平台，以显式点赞/点踩训练加权逻辑回归，结合 Scholar Maps 与主动学习破解冷启动，在 80 万评分数据集上 GTE-Large 达 AUC 86.75。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;咨询价值评估&quot;,&quot;咨询-行为交互&quot;,&quot;个性化搜索&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/similarity_value_consultation_value_assessment_and_alignment_for_personaliz/">Similarity = Value? Consultation Value Assessment and Alignment for Personalized Search</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>指出语义相似度无法代理咨询价值，提出场景范围/后验行为/时间衰减三维价值评估与 CAI 对齐的 VAPS，检索排序双任务全面领先。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL/EMNLP 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;RAG&quot;,&quot;多头早退&quot;,&quot;GCN 检索器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/the_efficiency_vs_accuracy_trade_off_optimizing_rag_enhanced_llm_recommende/">The Efficiency vs. Accuracy Trade-off: Optimizing RAG-Enhanced LLM Recommender Systems Using Multi-Head Early Exit</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 GCN 检索器替代 LLM embedder 加速 RAG，并以多头早退动态终止 LLM 推理，在 5 个 CTR 数据集上兼顾 AUC 与吞吐。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Contextual User Profiling&quot;,&quot;Persuasive Recommendation&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/agent_conversational_rec/towards_personalized_conversational_sales_agents_contextual_user_profiling_/">Towards Personalized Conversational Sales Agents: Contextual User Profiling for Strategic Action</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 CSALES 对话销售任务与 CSUSER 真实画像用户模拟器，CSI 智能体通过上下文画像驱动统一动作空间，SR 提升约 15-20pt、SWR 提升约 5-10pt。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;泛化能力评估&quot;,&quot;个性化推荐&quot;,&quot;熵拐点分析&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/user_behavior_prediction_as_a_generic_robust_scalable_and_low_cost_evaluati/">User Behavior Prediction as a Generic, Robust, Scalable, and Low-Cost Evaluation Strategy for Estimating Generalization in LLMs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出以用户行为预测（个性化）作为抗数据污染的 LLM 泛化评测范式，用熵-交叉熵拐点框架在电影/音乐推荐上量化 GPT-4o、GPT-4o-mini、Llama 的泛化边界。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;推理时扩展&quot;,&quot;分层规划&quot;,&quot;用户反馈对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/llm_rec/user_feedback_alignment_for_llm_powered_exploration_in_large_scale_recommen/">User Feedback Alignment for LLM-powered Exploration in Large-scale Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>解耦新颖性与用户对齐双 LLM，用集体隐式反馈训练对齐模型做 best-of-n 选择，线上同时提升新颖曝光与满意度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;权威偏见审计&quot;,&quot;反事实扰动&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/authority_bias_in_conversational_search_engines_for_academic_paper_recommen/">Authority Bias in Conversational Search Engines for Academic Paper Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>内容受控反事实审计揭示 LLM 学术推荐存在显著权威偏见，并提出 say-do gap 与前沿模型反向失效两大诊断现象。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2026 Findings&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;前缀微调&quot;,&quot;时序注意力&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/llm_rec/beyond_retrieval_learning_compact_user_representations_for_scalable_llm_per/">Beyond Retrieval: Learning Compact User Representations for Scalable LLM Personalization</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用双轨前缀（用户态+查询感知时序记录）替代提示序列化与逐用户适配器，LaMP 六任务全面领先且每用户参数降 130×</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;语义编码器&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/llm_rec/bridging_language_and_items_for_retrieval_and_recommendation_benchmarking_l/">Bridging Language and Items for Retrieval and Recommendation: Benchmarking LLMs as Semantic Encoders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建 BLaIR 基准，用 11 个 LLM 作语义编码器评测序列推荐、协同过滤与复杂查询商品搜索，揭示其排名与 MTEB 几乎不相关。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2026&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;用户聚类&quot;,&quot;个性化 RAG&quot;,&quot;HDBSCAN&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/collaborative_filtering/clusterrag_cluster_based_collaborative_filtering_for_personalized_retrieval/">ClusterRAG: Cluster-Based Collaborative Filtering for Personalized Retrieval-Augmented Generation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 HDBSCAN 将用户按画像嵌入聚类，簇内 ColBERTv2 排序选近邻，簇-文档两级检索融合协同画像做个性化 RAG，LaMP 全任务最优。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2026 Findings&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;信息茧房&quot;,&quot;模糊测试&quot;,&quot;立场检测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/content_fuzzing_for_escaping_information_cocoons_on_digital_social_media/">Content Fuzzing for Escaping Information Cocoons on Digital Social Media</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以立场检测置信度为灰盒反馈，用 LLM 迭代改写帖子翻转机器立场标签，助创作者突破信息茧房，跨 3 数据集 4 模型平均逃逸率 40%–91%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2026 Findings&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;持续图记忆&quot;,&quot;测试时自适应&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/graph_rec/continual_graph_memory_for_adaptive_recommendation_under_intent_drift/">Continual Graph Memory for Adaptive Recommendation under Intent Drift</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将知识图谱视为可写双记忆（语义图记忆+情景教训记忆），冻结参数下仅靠质量门控图编辑实现意图漂移自适应重排。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;贝叶斯偏好推断&quot;,&quot;主动提问&quot;,&quot;决策支持&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/agent_conversational_rec/decisive_guiding_user_decisions_with_optimal_preference_elicitation_from_un/">Decisive: Guiding User Decisions with Optimal Preference Elicitation from Unstructured Documents</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将决策解耦为文档客观打分矩阵与贝叶斯偏好推断，用决策感知信息增益主动提问，Top-1 准确率最高提升约 20%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2026 Industry&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;课程学习&quot;,&quot;GRPO 混合奖励&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/llm_rec/eager_enrich_and_align_generative_query_recommendation_from_clicked_items_i/">EAGER: Enrich-and-Align Generative Query Recommendation from Clicked Items in E-commerce Search</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>两阶段生成式 I2Q 查询推荐：课程化 SFT 扩意图覆盖 + GRPO 混合奖励对齐线上约束，线上 UCTR +0.83%、Pay Count +3.49%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2026 Findings&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;长期记忆基准&quot;,&quot;遗忘感知评估&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/agent_conversational_rec/from_recall_to_forgetting_benchmarking_long_term_memory_for_personalized_ag/">From Recall to Forgetting: Benchmarking Long-Term Memory for Personalized Agents</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 Memora 长周期记忆基准与 FAMA 遗忘感知指标，揭示 LLM 与记忆智能体在记忆突变下频繁复用失效记忆。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2026 Findings&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;LoRA 参数高效微调&quot;,&quot;图消息传递&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/llm_rec/graphlora_structure_aware_low_rank_adaptation_for_large_language_model_reco/">GraphLoRA: Structure-Aware Low-Rank Adaptation for Large Language Model Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将可训练 GNN 消息传递嵌入 LoRA 瓶颈（A 与 B 之间），让协同拓扑在参数空间传播；在 ML-1M/Amazon-Book 上以 r=8 的极小参数预算超越 CoRA 等 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;树搜索推理&quot;,&quot;分层偏好优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/agent_conversational_rec/harpo_hierarchical_agentic_reasoning_for_user_aligned_conversational_recomm/">HARPO: Hierarchical Agentic Reasoning for User-Aligned Conversational Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将对话推荐重构为多维质量驱动的结构化决策，用分层偏好奖励引导树搜索推理，ReDial R@10 达 29.8%（+18.7%）。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2026 Findings&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;分阶段兴趣演化&quot;,&quot;分组感知对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/llm_rec/hsuga_llm_enhanced_recommendation_with_hierarchical_semantic_understanding_/">HSUGA: LLM-Enhanced Recommendation with Hierarchical Semantic Understanding and Group-Aware Alignment</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用分阶段可编辑兴趣演化替代长序列一次性摘要，并按用户活跃度分组调节语义对齐强度，三数据集全库排序稳定提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2026&quot;,&quot;LLM推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;协同记忆图&quot;,&quot;架构解耦&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/llm_rec/memrec_collaborative_memory_augmented_agentic_recommender_system/">MemRec: Collaborative Memory-Augmented Agentic Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将记忆管理从推理中架构解耦，用轻量 LM_Mem 在协同记忆图上做零样本规则剪枝与异步传播，仅向重型 LLM_Rec 注入蒸馏后的高信号协同上下文，四基准全库排序 H@1 最高 +28.98%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;用户模拟&quot;,&quot;不确定性蒸馏&quot;,&quot;DPO&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/agent_conversational_rec/mirroring_users_towards_building_preference_aligned_user_simulator_with_use/">Mirroring Users: Towards Building Preference-aligned User Simulator with User Feedback in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 生成 EKB 决策过程作澄清、按认知不确定性蒸馏并拒绝采样去噪，微调 3B 模拟器在 8 域上超越 GPT-5 与教师模型。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2026 Findings&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;GEO 网页污染&quot;,&quot;检索增强鲁棒性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/llm_rec/one_polluted_page_is_enough_evaluating_web_content_pollution_in_llm_recomme/">One Polluted Page Is Enough: Evaluating Web Content Pollution in LLM Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 FORGE 基准，在冻结检索包上本地改写真实品牌为虚假品牌，量化 12 个 LLM 推荐器被 GEO 网页污染欺骗的比率，并揭示推理与防御的反效果。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2026&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;思维链推理&quot;,&quot;不确定性工具委派&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/multimodal_rec/reasonrec_a_reasoning_augmented_multimodal_agent_for_unified_recommendation/">ReasonRec: A Reasoning-Augmented Multimodal Agent for Unified Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将推荐重构为 Observe-Deliberate-Act 三阶段 CoT 推理智能体，以证据视界课程学习与不确定性引导工具委派，在五数据集四任务上 HR@5 相对提升超 30% 并降低推理延迟。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP Findings&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;MCP 工具编排&quot;,&quot;模糊意图&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-a-Judge&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/agent_conversational_rec/rectoolbench_benchmarking_recommendation_specific_tool_orchestration_under_/">RecToolBench: Benchmarking Recommendation-Specific Tool Orchestration under Fuzzy User Intent</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个基于 MCP 的模糊意图推荐工具编排基准，1288 条四级难度可执行任务，揭示语法合法≠推荐成功的执行-结果鸿沟。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;强化微调&quot;,&quot;奖励塑形&quot;,&quot;推理感知优势估计&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/llm_rec/rerec_reasoning_augmented_llm_based_recommendation_assistant_via_reinforcem/">ReRec: Reasoning-Augmented LLM-based Recommendation Assistant via Reinforcement Fine-tuning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用双图奖励塑形、推理感知优势估计与在线课程调度改造 RFT，使 3B LLM 在复杂查询推荐上大幅超越 GRPO 系基线并保留通用能力。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2026 Findings&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;全链路（输入/数据/模型/输出）&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec 公平性&quot;,&quot;二维分类法&quot;,&quot;评估协议&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/rethinking_fairness_in_llm_based_recommender_systems_a_survey/">Rethinking Fairness in LLM-Based Recommender Systems: A Survey</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个 LLM4Rec 公平性综述，提出「偏置机制×公平目标」二维分类法，系统梳理数据集、评估协议、缓解策略与可信交叉议题。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;元数据知识图谱&quot;,&quot;Top-K 位置奖励&quot;,&quot;结构感知重排&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/llm_rec/tatk_triple_aware_top_k_learning_with_knowledge_grounded_verification_for_l/">TATK: Triple-Aware Top-K Learning with Knowledge-Grounded Verification for LLM-based Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用元数据物品图贯穿提示接地、Top-K 位置奖励与推理期 top-M 重排，全库排序下 NDCG@10 最高提升 27.03%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;结构化上下文建模&quot;,&quot;蒙特卡洛树搜索&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/agent_conversational_rec/towards_effective_structured_context_modeling_for_conversational_recommende/">Towards Effective Structured Context Modeling for Conversational Recommender Systems via Dual-node Monte Carlo Tree Search</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将对话上下文建模为双节点 MCTS 树，ELNode 搜索追问动作、EXNode 精炼检索查询，Redial/OpenDialKG 上 SR 平均提升 9.07%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2026&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;标签增强检索&quot;,&quot;Token级新颖性估计&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/generative_rec/what_makes_an_ideal_quote_recommending_unexpected_yet_rational_quotations_v/">What Makes an Ideal Quote? Recommending &#x27;Unexpected yet Rational&#x27; Quotations via Novelty</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 NovelQR，用生成式标签智能体构建深层语义知识库并做标签增强检索，再以 token 级新颖性估计器缓解自回归续写偏差进行重排，双语数据集上人类评审 78% 偏好本系统。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;偏好对齐&quot;,&quot;自适应奖励边际&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/llm_rec/what_makes_llms_effective_sequential_recommenders_a_study_on_preference_int/">What Makes LLMs Effective Sequential Recommenders? A Study on Preference Intensity and Temporal Context</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示二元偏好对齐丢失偏好强度与时序上下文，提出自适应奖励边际 RecPO，五数据集 HR@1 全面领先 S-DPO。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;世界模型&quot;,&quot;反事实用户模拟&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/rl_bandit_rec/world_model_guided_reinforcement_learning_via_counterfactual_user_engagemen/">World Model-Guided Reinforcement Learning via Counterfactual User Engagement Simulation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将推荐视为动作、异构反馈视为观测，用语言世界模型 UEWM 做反事实用户模拟器，为 1.7B 策略提供同状态稠密奖励，跨域零样本超越大模型。</p>
</dd>
