<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACM MM 2023&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排/创意上线前预评估&quot;,&quot;多模态CTR预测&quot;,&quot;注意力热图归因&quot;,&quot;LLM广告内容分析&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACM_MM/2023/ctr_feature_interaction/against_opacity_explainable_ai_and_large_language_models_for_effective_digi/">Against Opacity: Explainable AI and Large Language Models for Effective Digital Advertising</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Multimedia">ACM MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 ViT 替换 ABN 构建 SoWide-v2 多模态三档 CTR 分类器，并用注意力热图与 GPT-3.5 流水线 SODA 为广告主侧提供可解释、可执行的创意优化洞察。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACM MM&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱提示&quot;,&quot;知识树掩码&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACM_MM/2023/sequential_rec/knowledge_prompt_tuning_for_sequential_recommendation/">Knowledge Prompt-tuning for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Multimedia">ACM MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将知识图谱经关系模板转成知识提示，并用知识树掩码去噪，与 T5 掩码个性化提示联合调优；Leave-One-Out + Full-Ranking 下 books NDCG@5 较 P5 提升 40.65%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式端到端&quot;,&quot;零样本提示&quot;,&quot;重复物品捷径&quot;,&quot;内容知识与协同知识解耦&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/agent_conversational_rec/large_language_models_as_zero_shot_conversational_recommenders/">Large Language Models as Zero-Shot Conversational Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用极简提示把 GPT-3.5/4、Vicuna、BAIZE 直接当零样本对话推荐器，揭露 CRS 评测中的「重复物品捷径」，并构建 63 万级 Reddit-Movie 真实数据集</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;文本匹配双塔&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;注意力稀疏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/sequential_rec/text_matching_improves_sequential_recommendation_by_reducing_popularity_bia/">Text Matching Improves Sequential Recommendation by Reducing Popularity Biases</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>TASTE 用模板将物品与交互历史文本化，经 T5 双塔文本匹配做下一物品推荐，配合会话切分注意力稀疏建模长历史，Full-Ranking 下平均提升约 18%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;LLM推荐&quot;,&quot;召回+精排&quot;,&quot;互补知识图&quot;,&quot;E-E-I权重决策&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/llm_rec/breaking_the_barrier_utilizing_large_language_models_for_industrial_recomme/">Breaking the Barrier: Utilizing Large Language Models for Industrial Recommendation Systems through an Inferential Knowledge Graph</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>CIKM 2024[LLM推荐] 用 LLM 离线构建互补知识图，结合 U2E2I 召回与 E-E-I 精排校准；在支付宝三场景 AUC 与线上转化显著提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;跨模态融合&quot;,&quot;GATE 门控&quot;,&quot;LoRA 两阶段训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/llm_rec/collaborative_cross_modal_fusion_with_large_language_model_for_recommendati/">Collaborative Cross-modal Fusion with Large Language Model for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以混合 Prompt 单 token 承载双模态，用 GATE 维度级门控融合 LLM 语义与 CF 协同嵌入，CTR 全量 AUC 相对 MF 提升 56.21%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;评分预测/精排&quot;,&quot;LoRA 插件&quot;,&quot;ID-内容对比对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/llm_rec/enhancing_content_based_recommendation_via_large_language_model/">Enhancing Content-based Recommendation via Large Language Model</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>CIKM 2024｜LLM4Rec：LoRA 跨域语义插件经 DARE 合并，再用 ID-内容对比对齐做评分预测；Amazon 11 域 8:1:1 划分下 MSE 显著优于 RGCL。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;上下文思维链 (In-context CoT)&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/llm_rec/large_language_models_enhanced_collaborative_filtering/">Large Language Models Enhanced Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>离线微调 RecGen-LLaMA 生成 CoT 推理构建 in-context 样例库，在线用轻量 Transformer 解码器蒸馏出“世界知识+推理引导的协同过滤特征”注入任意骨干模型，CTR 上 AUC RelaImpr 最高 7.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐与离散语义ID&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;可学习物品分词&quot;,&quot;RQ-VAE&quot;,&quot;协同正则&quot;,&quot;多样性正则&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/generative_rec/learnable_item_tokenization_for_generative_recommendation/">Learnable Item Tokenization for Generative Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>CIKM 2024｜生成式推荐与离散语义ID：LETTER 以 RQ-VAE 层级码、协同对比对齐和码本多样性正则学习物品标识符，并加排序引导生成损失；三数据集 LOO 全库排序 R@10 较 TIGER/LC-Rec 提升 4.7%–10.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;大语言模型零样本知识推理&quot;,&quot;分层元网络&quot;,&quot;Prompt 工程&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/cross_domain_multi_scenario/llm4msr_an_llm_enhanced_paradigm_for_multi_scenario_recommendation/">LLM4MSR: An LLM-Enhanced Paradigm for Multi-Scenario Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>冻结 LLM 以场景级/用户级 Prompt 零样本推理跨场景知识与个性化偏好，经分层元网络生成元层注入多场景 CTR 骨干，AUC 最高相对提升 40% 且推理仅 52ms。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;属性感知文本编码&quot;,&quot;多向量匹配 MaxSim&quot;,&quot;零样本迁移&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/sequential_rec/mars_matching_attribute_aware_representations_for_text_based_sequential_rec/">MARS: Matching Attribute-aware Representations for Text-based Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用属性级多向量文本编码 + 逐属性 MaxSim 匹配替代单一序列向量，在全库排序下 R@10 最高提升 24.43%，零样本迁移平均提升 13.11%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;记忆增强 LLM&quot;,&quot;实体记忆库&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/agent_conversational_rec/memocrs_memory_enhanced_sequential_conversational_recommender_systems_with_/">MemoCRS: Memory-enhanced Sequential Conversational Recommender Systems with Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用实体记忆库与通用记忆增强 LLM，建模跨会话偏好连续性；时序切分下 MRR@20 最高提升 38.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐与内容理解&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;跨模态对齐&quot;,&quot;PLM 文本知识&quot;,&quot;组合用药推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/multimodal_rec/natural_language_assisted_multi_modal_medication_recommendation/">Natural Language-Assisted Multi-modal Medication Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将组合用药推荐建模为患者-药物双模态对齐与相似度检索，用 PLM 抽取病历与药物文本知识，Jaccard 平均提升 4.72%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;视频摘要&quot;,&quot;视觉语言模型 (VLM)&quot;,&quot;偏好条件生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/multimodal_rec/personalized_video_summarization_by_multimodal_video_understanding/">Personalized Video Summarization by Multimodal Video Understanding</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将长视频文本化后按 genre 偏好做 T5 语义打分选场景，UserPrefSum F1 26.8 较 UniVTG 提升 13.6%，免训练、推理常数时间</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;离线评测&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-a-Judge&quot;,&quot;Serendipity&quot;,&quot;Prompt Engineering&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/a_universal_framework_for_offline_serendipity_evaluation_in_recommender_sys/">A Universal Framework for Offline Serendipity Evaluation in Recommender Systems via Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM-as-a-Judge 给推荐列表逐项打 1-5 级惊喜度分，绕开人工真值与阈值定义，实现跨数据集跨模型的离线惊喜度统一评测。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多语言相关性判别&quot;,&quot;任务自适应预训练&quot;,&quot;翻译增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/llm_rec/alibaba_international_e_commerce_product_search_competition_dcuragons_team_/">Alibaba International E-commerce Product Search Competition DcuRAGONs Team Technical Report</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 Gemma-3-12B 为骨干，用翻译增强+任务自适应预训练+类别感知查询分组划分，在阿里国际电商多语言相关性竞赛夺冠（F1_avg≈89.1）。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;多语言LLM&quot;,&quot;自蒸馏去噪&quot;,&quot;检索式查询增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/cold_start_rec/csrm_llm_embracing_multilingual_llms_for_cold_start_relevance_matching_in_e/">CSRM-LLM: Embracing Multilingual LLMs for Cold-Start Relevance Matching in Emerging E-commerce Markets</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>多语言 LLM 联合翻译任务、检索式查询增强与多轮自蒸馏，解决新兴市场零标注相关性匹配，离线 F1 0.965、线上缺陷率降 45.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;LVLM 嵌入&quot;,&quot;多模态原生表征&quot;,&quot;语义对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/multimodal_rec/do_recommender_systems_really_leverage_multimodal_content_a_comprehensive_a/">Do Recommender Systems Really Leverage Multimodal Content? A Comprehensive Analysis on Multimodal Representations for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用受控噪声实验证明多模态增益源于语义而非模型容量，并以 LVLM 结构化提示生成「原生多模态」[EOS] 嵌入替代晚期融合，Borda 聚合排名第一。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;文本协同过滤&quot;,&quot;缩放定律&quot;,&quot;通用物品表征&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/llm_rec/exploring_the_upper_limits_of_text_based_collaborative_filtering_using_larg/">Exploring the Upper Limits of Text-Based Collaborative Filtering Using Large Language Models: Discoveries and Insights</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 TCF 物品编码器从 125M 一路放大到 175B，系统检验缩放极限、通用表征、对 IDCF 的竞争力与零样本迁移四项命题。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与POI推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec&quot;,&quot;群体推荐&quot;,&quot;QLoRA适配器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/harnessing_large_language_models_for_group_poi_recommendations/">Harnessing Large Language Models for Group POI Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用语义增强 POI Token + QLoRA 双适配器 + 行程目的自监督，破解群体 POI 推荐偏好多样与数据极稀疏难题，HR@10 平均提升 4.4%–9.0%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/投放策略&quot;,&quot;LLM Agent&quot;,&quot;Contextual Bandit&quot;,&quot;关键词剪枝&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/rl_bandit_rec/kp_agent_keyword_pruning_in_sponsored_search_advertising_via_llm_powered_co/">KP-Agent: Keyword Pruning in Sponsored Search Advertising via LLM-Powered Contextual Bandits</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将关键词剪枝建模为上下文赌博机，用 LLM Agent 生成代码片段执行剪枝，美团真实数据上累计利润最高提升 49.28%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;线性自编码器&quot;,&quot;闭式解知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;长尾推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/llm_rec/llm_enhanced_linear_autoencoders_for_recommendation/">LLM-Enhanced Linear Autoencoders for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 语义矩阵构造 item-item 相关阵并以知识蒸馏正则注入 EASE 闭式解，全库排序下 R@20 平均提升 27.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Decision Transformer&quot;,&quot;Expectile 回归&quot;,&quot;LLM 先验&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/rl_bandit_rec/maximum_in_support_return_modeling_for_dynamic_recommendation_with_language/">Maximum In-Support Return Modeling for Dynamic Recommendation with Language Model Prior</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将轨迹拼接从训练期移至动作推理期，用 expectile 回归估计最大在支撑回报并自适应截断历史，配合 LLM 先验初始化 DT，在 5 个离线数据集与 VirtualTB 仿真上全面超越 EDT4Rec。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;软提示调优&quot;,&quot;用户画像推断&quot;,&quot;向量量化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/llm_rec/prompt_tuning_as_user_inherent_profile_inference_machine/">Prompt Tuning as User Inherent Profile Inference Machine</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将用户潜在画像建模为可训练软提示，用因果掩码+EM 推断、码本量化成协同 ID，AUC 最高相对提升 4.77%，已上线华为应用市场。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;特征选择&quot;,&quot;桥接向量&quot;,&quot;提示迭代&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/llm_rec/self_surrogate_light_feature_selection_with_large_language_models_in_deep_r/">SELF: Surrogate-light Feature Selection with Large Language Models in Deep Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用多 LLM 迭代提示生成语义特征排序，再以可学习桥接向量对齐任务空间，实现轻量特征选择，AUC 全面领先且线上 CTR +3.01%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;慢思考推理&quot;,&quot;GRPO 强化学习&quot;,&quot;用户模拟&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/agent_conversational_rec/starec_an_efficient_agent_framework_for_recommender_systems_via_autonomous_/">STARec: An Efficient Agent Framework for Recommender Systems via Autonomous Deliberate Reasoning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将每位用户建模为具备快/慢双认知的 LLM Agent，用 SFT 蒸馏 + GRPO 排序奖励两阶段训练，仅用 0.4% 数据即在 ML-1M/CDs 上超越全量训练基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;课程学习&quot;,&quot;知识图谱融合&quot;,&quot;前缀提示学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/agent_conversational_rec/step_stepwise_curriculum_learning_for_context_knowledge_fusion_in_conversat/">STEP: Stepwise Curriculum Learning for Context-Knowledge Fusion in Conversational Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用三阶段课程学习驱动 F-Former 对齐对话与知识图谱，再以双前缀提示注入冻结 PLM，ReDial R@1 提升 15.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLM 知识抽取&quot;,&quot;语义-行为对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/llm_rec/trawl_external_knowledge_enhanced_recommendation_with_llm_assistance/">TRAWL: External Knowledge-Enhanced Recommendation with LLM Assistance</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 从原始外部文本抽取推荐知识，再以对比学习适配器对齐语义与行为空间，ML-1M 上 AUC 稳定提升且微信线上 A/B 三项指标正向。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;LLM User Simulator&quot;,&quot;Trait Interference&quot;,&quot;Page-level Quality Anchor&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/agent_conversational_rec/a_behavioral_trait_leaks_into_preferences_diagnosing_trait_interference_in_/">A Behavioral Trait Leaks into Preferences: Diagnosing Trait Interference in LLM User Simulators</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 LLM 用户模拟器中活跃度特质干扰偏好边界并导致评估失效，提出页面级质量锚点 PQA 恢复特质独立性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;负迁移抑制&quot;,&quot;双专家门控&quot;,&quot;物品级对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/cross_domain_multi_scenario/a_dual_expert_strategy_integrating_llms_to_mitigate_negative_transfer_in_cr/">A Dual-Expert Strategy Integrating LLMs to Mitigate Negative Transfer in Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用域门控双专家+物品级注意力掩码+双池负采样对比学习，抑制 LLM 跨域序列推荐中的负迁移，10 域上超越 26 个基线，线上 CTR 相对提升 47.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;熵减奖励&quot;,&quot;多轮偏好引导&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/agent_conversational_rec/ask_to_be_sure_informative_interactions_for_confident_multi_turn_llm_recomm/">Ask to Be Sure: Informative Interactions for Confident Multi-Turn LLM Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以推荐列表采样熵的下降量作为信息增益奖励，免真值微调 LLM 多轮对话策略，INSPIRED/ReDial 上 Hit 与对话效率双升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;转化归因&quot;,&quot;触点选择&quot;,&quot;LLM语义推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/ctr_feature_interaction/can_large_language_models_identify_meaningful_touchpoints_in_conversion_att/">Can Large Language Models Identify Meaningful Touchpoints in Conversion Attribution?</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建SILVA触点选择基准，揭示隐式触点语义鸿沟，系统评测8个LLM的成对/列表提示能力，并以LLM归因标签增强工业CVR训练获+0.35pp GAUC。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;合成查询生成&quot;,&quot;构造式相关性标签&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/cold_start_rec/castle_contrastive_and_seed_guided_training_for_cold_start_natural_language/">CASTLE: Contrastive and Seed-Guided Training for Cold-Start Natural Language Search</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用预订会话对比房源对+用户调研种子查询联合生成合成查询与构造式相关性标签，KL 1.01 对齐真实用户，9.2× 优于基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回前过滤&quot;,&quot;内容过时性&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;多模态流量预测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/decomposing_staleness_in_recommender_systems_a_dual_filter_framework_for_su/">Decomposing Staleness in Recommender Systems: A Dual-Filter Framework for Supersession and Decay</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将推荐内容过时性拆解为“取代”与“衰减”双机制，用 LLM 蒸馏成对分类器与多模态流量比预测器做上游过滤，Google Discover 两年部署使过时举报降 54.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;置信度校准&quot;,&quot;目录忠实性幻觉&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/llm_rec/do_llm_recommenders_know_when_they_re_hallucinating_auditing_confidence_cal/">Do LLM Recommenders Know When They&#x27;re Hallucinating? Auditing Confidence Calibration in Catalog Faithfulness</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>联合审计零样本 LLM 推荐的目录幻觉率与言语化置信度校准，揭示校准误差方向由目录而非模型决定，并给出匹配器验证协议。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;隐式推理&quot;,&quot;逐步推理损失&quot;,&quot;语义ID生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/llm_rec/do_recommenders_need_to_think_in_words_latent_reasoning_for_efficient_llm_b/">Do Recommenders Need to Think in Words? Latent Reasoning for Efficient LLM-Based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用训练期逐步头监督替代推理期 CoT/隐式推理，HR@10 相对提升 4.0–5.0% 且延迟降低 18.3×</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;反馈回路仿真&quot;,&quot;LLM幻觉与偏见诊断&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/echotrace_diagnosing_recursive_risks_in_llm_powered_recommender_systems/">EchoTrace: Diagnosing Recursive Risks in LLM-Powered Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出角色感知三阶段诊断框架，用受控反馈回路仿真追踪 LLM 偏见与幻觉在 LLM4RS 中的递归放大与生态极化。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;反事实解释&quot;,&quot;LLM 摘要&quot;,&quot;DPO 偏好优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/agent_conversational_rec/from_counterfactual_edits_to_human_narratives_llm_driven_interpretations_fo/">From Counterfactual Edits to Human Narratives: LLM-Driven Interpretations for Recommender System Explanations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 将冗长反事实编辑列表摘要为高层偏好叙事，并以人类校准指标评估正确性/信息量/语言质量。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-Judge&quot;,&quot;语义ID&quot;,&quot;离线评估指标&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/llm_based_user_product_relevance_evaluation_for_dynamic_product_advertising/">LLM-based User-Product Relevance Evaluation for Dynamic Product Advertising</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 SID 分组意图压缩 + 智能体指南蒸馏小模型，构建无查询的 DPA 召回离线相关性指标，与线上 CP/CR/CVR 强对齐。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;序列推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;归一化流&quot;,&quot;条件变分自编码器&quot;,&quot;LLM 语义融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/sequential_rec/lsf_sr_latent_semantic_fusion_for_sequential_recommendation_via_flow_based_/">LSF-SR: Latent Semantic Fusion for Sequential Recommendation via Flow-based Conditional Variational Autoencoders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用归一化流增强的 CVAE 将 LLM 语义作为条件概率融合进 ID 协同嵌入，Full-Ranking 下 Recall@20 最高提升 12.98%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;物品画像&quot;,&quot;上下文感知特征选择&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/llm_rec/profiling_what_matters_context_aware_item_profiles_from_large_scale_metadat/">Profiling What Matters: Context-Aware Item Profiles from Large-Scale Metadata for LLM Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将海量异构物品元数据结构化并训练轻量选择器，为每个用户-物品对动态生成上下文相关画像，显著提升 LLM 重排效果。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;LLM 重排器&quot;,&quot;评估协议敏感性&quot;,&quot;解码稳定性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/agent_conversational_rec/retrieval_scoring_and_decoding_shape_performance_and_stability_in_llm_based/">Retrieval, Scoring, and Decoding Shape Performance and Stability in LLM-based Conversational Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在 ReDial 上系统解耦候选池规模、一阶段检索器与解码温度对 LLM 重排器的影响，揭示协议选择可移动 NDCG@10 超过模型差距。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;自蒸馏&quot;,&quot;推理增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/llm_rec/selfdr_self_distillation_from_reasoning_for_llm_based_recommendation/">SelfDR: Self-Distillation from Reasoning for LLM-Based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用下游推荐奖励训练同源 Reasoner 生成理由，构建推理增强教师，再自蒸馏出免推理的直接推荐学生，重排 HR@1 提升约 15%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;Recall Ceiling&quot;,&quot;评估协议&quot;,&quot;闭候选重排&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/llm_rec/the_recall_ceiling_of_llm_recommendation_reranking/">The Recall Ceiling of LLM Recommendation Reranking</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>证明闭候选重排器 NDCG 受自身窗口召回率硬上界约束，Oracle 协议高估 92–95%，九类 LLM 与监督重排器在真实检索下均无法显著超越 CF。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;LLM推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;意图推理&quot;,&quot;个性化稠密检索&quot;,&quot;GRPO&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/llm_rec/think_to_personalize_unifying_reasoning_and_retrieval_for_user_centric_pers/">Think-to-Personalize: Unifying Reasoning and Retrieval for User-Centric Personalized Dense Retrieval</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 SFT+GRPO 两阶段让 LLM 显式推理用户历史生成意图增强查询并统一编码，Full-Ranking 下 Broad/LongTail 召回显著提升，线上订单量 +0.46%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;协同视角内容理解&quot;,&quot;GRPO 强化学习精炼&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/llm_rec/towards_comprehensible_recommendation_with_large_language_model_fine_tuning/">Towards Comprehensible Recommendation with Large Language Model Fine-tuning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 GRPO 两阶段对齐 LLM 生成协同视角的用户模式与推荐理由，并以推荐模型打分为奖励精炼，Full-Ranking 下 Recall@5 相对提升 14–18%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;持续推荐&quot;,&quot;SPC 三难困境&quot;,&quot;Procrustes 投影&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/llm_rec/tracer_balancing_stability_plasticity_cognitivity_trilemma_for_llm_enhanced/">TRACER: Balancing Stability-Plasticity-Cognitivity Trilemma for LLM Enhanced Continual Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 LLM 增强持续推荐中的 SPC 三难困境，用 Procrustes 投影、置信门控凝聚与语义同步三模块协同破局，Full-Ranking 下 ACC 最高提升 14.38%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;免训练推荐&quot;,&quot;协同信号注入&quot;,&quot;图传播表征细化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/llm_rec/training_free_llm_based_recommendation_with_post_llm_item_refinement_using_/">Training-Free LLM-Based Recommendation with Post-LLM Item Refinement Using Collaborative Signals</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 post-LLM 范式 CoRRe，用共购图方向细化与流行度幅度校准改造 LLM 物品表征，免训练即在三域 Amazon 上超越全部免训练基线并多数超越训练式方法。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLM 常识知识图谱&quot;,&quot;互信息最大化对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2024/graph_rec/common_sense_enhanced_knowledge_based_recommendation_with_large_language_mo/">Common Sense Enhanced Knowledge-based Recommendation with Large Language Model</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 ChatGPT 离线生成类目互补/替代常识图，并以互信息最大化对齐硬合并失败的两张知识图，为 CKE/CFKG 带来最高 8.5% 的 nDCG 提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;LoRA 指令微调&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2024/llm_rec/unlocking_the_potential_of_large_language_models_for_explainable_recommenda/">Unlocking the Potential of Large Language Models for Explainable Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出两阶段框架 LLMXRec：推荐模型与 LLM 解释生成器解耦，用 LoRA 指令微调与个性化提示生成可解释理由，并以判别器、人工打分、属性预测三视角评估，胜率最高提升 31.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;知识提示 (Knowledge Prompt)&quot;,&quot;推理提示 (Reasoning Prompt)&quot;,&quot;内容增强语言建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2025/sequential_rec/reformulating_sequential_recommendation_learning_dynamic_user_interest_with/">Reformulating Sequential Recommendation: Learning Dynamic User Interest with Content-enriched Language Modeling</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用知识提示+推理提示将序列推荐改写为文本生成任务，GPT-2 冻结主干下在三个数据集全排序协议上取得 Recall@5 相对提升约 5%–12%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;课程强化学习&quot;,&quot;解释一致性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2026/agent_conversational_rec/curr_rlcer_curriculum_reinforcement_learning_for_coherence_explainable_reco/">Curr-RLCER: Curriculum Reinforcement Learning For Coherence Explainable Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用三阶段课程强化学习（CTR→评分→解释生成）训练 LLM，并设计一致性奖励与评估方案，使解释与评分双向对齐。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;偏好优化&quot;,&quot;动态负采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2026/llm_rec/dynamicpo_dynamic_preference_optimization_for_recommendation/">DynamicPO: Dynamic Preference Optimization for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示多负样本 DPO 的偏好优化坍缩现象，提出动态边界负样本选择与双边际动态 β 调整，即插即用提升 LLM 推荐 HitRatio@1。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA 2026&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;分层强化学习&quot;,&quot;LLM 语义规划&quot;,&quot;过滤气泡缓解&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2026/rl_bandit_rec/llm_enhanced_reinforcement_learning_for_long_term_user_satisfaction_in_inte/">LLM-Enhanced Reinforcement Learning for Long-Term User Satisfaction in Interactive Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>LLM 做类别级语义规划、PPO 做物品级策略，KuaiSim 全会话仿真下 Tint/Rcum 显著超越 DNaIR 等 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2022&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;零样本提示&quot;,&quot;语言模型似然&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2022/llm_rec/zero_shot_recommendation_as_language_modeling/">Zero-Shot Recommendation as Language Modeling</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>冻结 GPT-2，用 “Movies like ⟨m1⟩,…,⟨mi⟩” 提示的序列似然对物品零样本打分；MovieLens-1M 上 MAP@1 在训练用户少于 50/100 时反超 BPR。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2024&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;LLaMA-LoRA&quot;,&quot;指令化序列模板&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2024/llm_rec/genrec_large_language_model_for_generative_recommendation/">GenRec: Large Language Model for Generative Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLaMA-LoRA 将用户交互历史改写为指令序列，直接生成下一物品名称而非逐候选打分；MovieLens-25M 上 HR@5 相对 P5 提升 50.3%，但稀疏 Amazon-Toys 上反而下降。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2024&quot;,&quot;LLM推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;Zero-Shot Ranking&quot;,&quot;Prompting &amp; Bootstrapping&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2024/llm_rec/large_language_models_are_zero_shot_rankers_for_recommender_systems/">Large Language Models are Zero-Shot Rankers for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将推荐形式化为条件排序，用提示与自举缓解顺序/位置/流行度偏差，在 ML-1M/Games 的 20 候选零样本排序中显著优于 UniSRec。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐与离散语义ID&quot;,&quot;生成式端到端&quot;,&quot;检索增强生成&quot;,&quot;话题标签推荐&quot;,&quot;难负样本对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2024/generative_rec/right_retrieval_augmented_generation_for_mainstream_hashtag_recommendation/">RIGHT: Retrieval-augmented Generation for Mainstream Hashtag Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用检索器+选择器+生成器的检索增强框架做主流话题标签推荐，在 Twitter/Weibo 两数据集上 F1@1 最高提升约 10.8%，并可零样本注入 ChatGPT 带来 12.7%/18.3% 增益。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;描述性货架&quot;,&quot;LLM 元数据增强&quot;,&quot;列表解释&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2025/llm_rec/contextualizing_spotify_s_audiobook_list_recommendations_with_descriptive_s/">Contextualizing Spotify&#x27;s Audiobook List Recommendations with Descriptive Shelves</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 抽取十类书目描述符并生成多样化描述性货架标题，两轮线上 A/B 测试验证发现性与互动指标提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2025&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多语种句子编码器&quot;,&quot;零样本跨语言迁移&quot;,&quot;领域适配&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2025/cold_start_rec/news_without_borders_domain_adaptation_of_multilingual_sentence_embeddings_/">News Without Borders: Domain Adaptation of Multilingual Sentence Embeddings for Cross-lingual News Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建 PolyNews 多语新闻语料，以 DAE+MT 领域适配 LaBSE 得 NaSE，冻结嵌入+晚期点击融合在 ZS-XLT 冷启动新闻推荐上达 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;新闻推荐&quot;,&quot;语言模型编码器&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2025/llm_rec/revisiting_language_models_in_neural_news_recommender_systems/">Revisiting Language Models in Neural News Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在 MIND 上统一复现 8 种 LM 作为新闻编码器，发现更大模型不必然更优，但显著利好冷启动用户。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;LLM 标签抽取&quot;,&quot;RAG&quot;,&quot;工业级部署&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2025/llm_rec/text2playlist_generating_personalized_playlists_from_text_on_deezer/">Text2Playlist: Generating Personalized Playlists from Text on Deezer</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 抽取显隐式标签 + CF 亲和度重排 + LLM 列表精炼，将宽意图文本查询转为个性化歌单，Deezer 线上 20% 灰度中生成歌单后续收听率达 45% vs 人工 27%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;LLM Reranking&quot;,&quot;外部推荐器适配&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2026/agent_conversational_rec/improving_conversational_recommendation_with_contextual_adaptation_of_exter/">Improving Conversational Recommendation with Contextual Adaptation of External Recommenders and LLM-based Reranking</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用轻量实体级序列推荐器产出候选、LLM 做上下文适配重排，ReDial/INSPIRED 上 H@5 平均提升 54%/25%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;数据合成/仿真训练&quot;,&quot;Reference-Free 仿真&quot;,&quot;角色掩码损失&quot;,&quot;双智能体交互&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2026/agent_conversational_rec/interplay_training_independent_simulators_for_reference_free_conversational/">Interplay: Training Independent Simulators for Reference-Free Conversational Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>训练独立用户/推荐双模拟器，以目标属性替代目标物品消除 oracle 泄漏，8B 模型 Recall@1 超 32B 基线 15 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2026&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLM 提示去噪&quot;,&quot;后训练用户画像清洗&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2026/self_supervised_denoising/post_training_denoising_of_user_profiles_with_llms_in_collaborative_filteri/">Post-Training Denoising of User Profiles with LLMs in Collaborative Filtering Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 零样本/少样本提示在推理前清洗用户画像，无需重训 CF 模型即获最高 13% NDCG/HR 提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;用户画像蒸馏&quot;,&quot;序列推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2026/llm_rec/pre_trained_llms_meet_sequential_recommenders_efficient_user_centric_knowle/">Pre-trained LLMs Meet Sequential Recommenders: Efficient User-Centric Knowledge Distillation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用预训练 LLM 生成文本用户画像并蒸馏进 SASRec/BERT4Rec 隐层，两阶段训练实现零推理开销的语义增强，Recall@10 最高提升 23.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;自然语言用户画像&quot;,&quot;学术文献推荐基准&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2026/llm_rec/scinup_natural_language_user_interest_profiles_for_scientific_literature_re/">SciNUP: Natural Language User Interest Profiles for Scientific Literature Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建千名作者的自然语言兴趣画像学术推荐基准，系统对比稀疏/稠密/LLM重排，RRF 融合达 NDCG@10 0.4481。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;用户模拟&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-a-Judge&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2026/agent_conversational_rec/usersimcrs_v2_simulation_based_evaluation_for_conversational_recommender_sy/">UserSimCRS v2: Simulation-Based Evaluation for Conversational Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>升级对话推荐用户模拟评测工具包，引入 LLM 模拟器、统一数据格式与 LLM-as-a-Judge 指标，案例揭示模拟器间系统排序显著分歧。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;语义ID量化&quot;,&quot;LLM指令微调&quot;,&quot;生成式检索&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2024/generative_rec/adapting_large_language_models_by_integrating_collaborative_semantics_for_r/">Adapting Large Language Models by Integrating Collaborative Semantics for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>RQ-VAE 层级语义索引 + Sinkhorn 均匀映射消除索引冲突，LLaMA-7B 多任务对齐微调实现全库生成式检索，全排序平均提升 25.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/表征增强&quot;,&quot;LLM 图增强&quot;,&quot;去噪鲁棒训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2024/llm_rec/llmrec_large_language_models_with_graph_augmentation_for_recommendation/">LLMRec: Large Language Models with Graph Augmentation for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>LLM 从自然语言侧增强 U-I 交互边、物品属性与用户画像，并以噪声剪枝+MAE 鲁棒化，全库排序 R@10 提升 13.95%/4.88%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;表征解耦对齐&quot;,&quot;结构对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2025/llm_rec/darec_a_disentangled_alignment_framework_for_large_language_model_and_recom/">DaRec: A Disentangled Alignment Framework for Large Language Model and Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>从信息论证明零间隙对齐次优，提出解耦共享/特有表征+全局局部结构对齐的即插即用框架，全库排序下 Recall@20 最高提升 5.33%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;软提示蒸馏&quot;,&quot;序列推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2025/llm_rec/delrec_distilling_sequential_pattern_to_enhance_llms_based_sequential_recom/">DELRec: Distilling Sequential Pattern to Enhance LLMs-based Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用软提示两阶段蒸馏传统序列模型的时间感知与推荐模式，再微调 LLM 做序列推荐，四数据集 HR/NDCG 全面超越 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;营销知识图谱构建&quot;,&quot;渐进式提示增强&quot;,&quot;教师蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2025/llm_rec/making_large_language_models_better_knowledge_miners_for_online_marketing_w/">Making Large Language Models Better Knowledge Miners for Online Marketing with Progressive Prompting Augmentation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用渐进式提示增强把 LLM 变成可控的营销知识图谱挖掘器，并以教师蒸馏出可部署的 LightPAIR，受众定向覆盖提升 15%–122%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2026&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;GRPO 行为对齐&quot;,&quot;自适应门控融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2026/multimodal_rec/gala_generative_aligned_learning_for_adaptive_multimodal_representation_in_/">GALA: Generative Aligned Learning for Adaptive Multimodal Representation in the Taobao Shangou Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>三阶段流水线：行为三元组预训练 + GRPO 生成式行为对齐 + 自适应门控融合，离线 AUC +0.12/+0.20，线上订单量 +0.55%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;查询改写&quot;,&quot;GRPO 强化学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2026/llm_rec/relevance_matters_a_multi_task_and_multi_stage_large_language_model_approac/">Relevance Matters: A Multi-Task and Multi-Stage Large Language Model Approach for E-commerce Query Rewriting</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将相关性打标任务注入电商查询改写，多任务 SFT + 查询级归一化 GRPO 融合奖励对齐，离线召回 67.94%、线上 UCVR +0.181%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2023&quot;,&quot;冷启动与长尾推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;零样本推荐&quot;,&quot;多模态语义嵌入&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2023/cold_start_rec/zero_shot_recommendations_with_pre_trained_large_language_models_for_multim/">Zero-Shot Recommendations with Pre-Trained Large Language Models for Multimodal Nudging</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多模态内容文本化并用LLM语义嵌入做零样本匹配，合成屏幕时间干预场景Top-5推荐中83%合适、8%不合适。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2024&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;LLM 用户画像重建&quot;,&quot;模型无关知识迁移&quot;,&quot;RecBole 实验框架&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2024/llm_rec/llm_kt_a_versatile_framework_for_knowledge_transfer_from_large_language_mod/">LLM-KT: A Versatile Framework for Knowledge Transfer from Large Language Models to Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 LLM 生成的用户画像注入任意 CF 模型的中间层并以重建为辅助任务做知识迁移，模型无关、基于 RecBole 的插件式框架，ML-1M/Amazon CDs 上 NDCG@10 最高 +21.57%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2025&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;逆强化学习&quot;,&quot;模仿学习&quot;,&quot;离线 RL&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2025/rl_bandit_rec/beyond_static_llm_policies_imitation_enhanced_reinforcement_learning_for_re/">Beyond Static LLM Policies: Imitation-Enhanced Reinforcement Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 轨迹作次优示范，经对抗逆强化学习提取奖励并加权模仿，离线 RL 策略在 Steam/Amazon 上累积奖励较 BiLLP 提升 13%+。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;双层神经架构搜索&quot;,&quot;零样本 LLM 解释生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2025/agent_conversational_rec/bi_nas_towards_effective_and_personalized_explanation_for_recommender_syste/">Bi-NAS: Towards Effective and Personalized Explanation for Recommender Systems via Bi-Level Neural Architecture Search</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用双层 NAS 自动搜索交叉注意力与特征交互算子，再以零样本 LLM 提示生成个性化解释，四数据集上推荐与解释指标双优。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;推荐遗忘&quot;,&quot;RAG&quot;,&quot;遗忘偏置 (Unlearning Bias)&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/customized_retrieval_augmented_generation_with_llm_for_debiasing_recommenda/">Customized Retrieval-Augmented Generation with LLM for Debiasing Recommendation Unlearning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将推荐遗忘重构为 RAG 检索端过滤问题，用三种检索策略实现用户级原子遗忘，平均加速 4.5× 且保留约 90% 原模型效用。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;时序用户画像&quot;,&quot;注意力融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2025/llm_rec/effectiveness_of_llms_in_temporal_user_profiling_for_recommendation/">Effectiveness of LLMs in Temporal User Profiling for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 分别生成短期/长期自然语言画像，经 SBERT 编码与注意力融合做内容推荐，Movies&amp;TV 上 Recall@10 相对提升 17%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;上下文压缩&quot;,&quot;个性化专家&quot;,&quot;KV缓存&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2025/sequential_rec/efficient_sequential_recommendation_for_long_term_user_interest_via_persona/">Efficient Sequential Recommendation for Long Term User Interest Via Personalization</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用可学习 token 将长历史分段压缩为个性化专家，配合定制注意力掩码与 KV 缓存，在 HSTU/HLLM 上以约 1/4 推理开销逼近全序列精度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;Agentic Copilot&quot;,&quot;主动学习人格分类&quot;,&quot;可解释推理链&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2025/agent_conversational_rec/persopilot_an_adaptive_ai_copilot_for_transparent_contextualized_persona_cl/">PersoPilot: An Adaptive AI-Copilot for Transparent Contextualized Persona Classification and Personalized Response Generation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>双模式智能体 Copilot：PersoAgent 做上下文人格抽取与社区推荐，Analytic Tools 用 Phi-4-mini+TF-IDF 主动学习闭环做可解释人格分类，UniEval 四维均分 0.84–1.0。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;知识图谱增强LLM&quot;,&quot;可解释偏好摘要&quot;,&quot;图实体描述预训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2025/agent_conversational_rec/reasoning_over_user_preferences_knowledge_graph_augmented_llms_for_explaina/">Reasoning over User Preferences: Knowledge Graph-Augmented LLMs for Explainable Conversational Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>COMPASS 用图实体描述预训练+知识感知指令微调，让 LLM 生成可解释文本偏好摘要并以门控注入现有 CRS，ReDial/INSPIRED 上 HR@10 最高提升 44.76%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;小语言模型蒸馏&quot;,&quot;倒置强化学习&quot;,&quot;多模态提示生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2025/llm_rec/scaling_multimodal_search_and_recommendation_with_small_language_models_via/">Scaling Multimodal Search and Recommendation with Small Language Models via Upside-Down Reinforcement Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Llama-3 合成 5200 万意图-提示对蒸馏进 104M nanoGPT，并以倒置强化学习实现长度/模态可控的多任务提示生成，性能逼近 8B 模型而显存仅 500MB。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLM Agent 自主科研&quot;,&quot;多智能体脚手架&quot;,&quot;表征学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2026/llm_rec/autoresearch_at_production_scale_failure_modes_and_a_multi_agent_framework/">AutoResearch at Production Scale: Failure Modes and a Multi-Agent Framework</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 LLM 自主科研范式落地生产级推荐表征学习，归纳五大失效模式并提出 prevent/persist/redirect 三智能体脚手架，Recall@6 提升 1.82 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;层间出口融合&quot;,&quot;自适应连续路由&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2026/llm_rec/empowering_compact_llms_with_fusion_of_layer_wise_exits_for_recommendation/">Empowering Compact LLMs with Fusion of Layer-wise Exits for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在紧凑 LLM 每层插入预测出口并用连续 ReLU 路由器自适应融合多层打分分布，target-k hinge 损失约束激活出口数，全库排序下精度超越大模型基线且延迟仅增 &lt;1ms。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;几何记忆&quot;,&quot;轨迹条件重建&quot;,&quot;非均匀度量&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2026/llm_rec/memory_has_geometry_non_uniform_geometric_memory_for_long_horizon_personali/">Memory Has Geometry: Non-Uniform Geometric Memory for Long-Horizon Personalized AI</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将长程个性化记忆重构为用户特定非均匀几何状态空间上的轨迹条件状态估计，替代扁平记录检索范式。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;序列推荐&quot;,&quot;精排/生成式端到端&quot;,&quot;Instance-wise LoRA&quot;,&quot;Mixture of Experts&quot;,&quot;负迁移&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/sequential_rec/customizing_language_models_with_instance_wise_lora_for_sequential_recommen/">Customizing Language Models with Instance-wise LoRA for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 LoRA 拆成 MoE 专家并由序列表征门控，实现实例级参数定制，HR@1 平均相对 LoRA 提升 11.4% 且可训练参数不增。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/下一物品预测&quot;,&quot;双视图语义协同融合&quot;,&quot;检索增强自蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/sequential_rec/llm_esr_large_language_models_enhancement_for_long_tailed_sequential_recomm/">LLM-ESR: Large Language Models Enhancement for Long-tailed Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用冻结的 LLM 物品/用户语义嵌入搭建语义-协同双视图与相似用户检索自蒸馏，在不增加 LLM 推理开销的前提下同时缓解长尾物品与长尾用户难题。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS 2024&quot;,&quot;LLM推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;DPO 偏好对齐&quot;,&quot;Softmax 负采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/llm_rec/on_softmax_direct_preference_optimization_for_recommendation/">On Softmax Direct Preference Optimization for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将成对 DPO 推广为多负样本 softmax 排序损失 S-DPO，理论桥接负采样 softmax 并自带难负样本挖掘，三数据集 HR@1 较次优提升 11.1%–47.0%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;LLM Agent&quot;,&quot;推荐系统基准&quot;,&quot;文本环境仿真&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/agent_conversational_rec/agentrecbench_benchmarking_llm_agent_based_personalized_recommender_systems/">AgentRecBench: Benchmarking LLM Agent-based Personalized Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个 LLM 智能体推荐系统基准，构建 U-R-I 文本交互环境与三类场景，揭示结构化工作流智能体显著优于传统与朴素 Agent。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;语义标识符&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;推理效率&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/llm_rec/can_llms_outshine_conventional_recommenders_a_comparative_evaluation/">Can LLMs Outshine Conventional Recommenders? A Comparative Evaluation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>RecBench 系统对比 17 个 LLM 与 DLRM 在 4 种物品表征、CTR/SeqRec 双任务上的精度-效率权衡，LLM 精度领先但推理慢千倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;上下文引导解码&quot;,&quot;免训练个性化&quot;,&quot;贝叶斯推断&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/llm_rec/context_steering_controllable_personalization_at_inference_time/">Context Steering: Controllable Personalization at Inference Time</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>免训练解码期个性化：用有/无上下文两次前向的似然差线性缩放上下文影响力，实现可控个性化推荐与贝叶斯意图反演。</p>
</dd>
