<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;投机解码&quot;,&quot;Top-K 对齐&quot;,&quot;推理加速&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/generative_rec/efficient_inference_for_large_language_model_based_generative_recommendatio/">Efficient Inference for Large Language Model-based Generative Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将投机解码引入 LLM 生成式推荐，提出 N-to-K 验证下的 AtSpeed-S/R 对齐目标与松弛采样验证，实现近 2×–2.5× 无损/近似无损加速。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;向量量化&quot;,&quot;对比对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/llm_rec/face_a_general_framework_for_mapping_collaborative_filtering_embeddings_int/">FACE: A General Framework for Mapping Collaborative Filtering Embeddings into LLM Tokens</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用解耦投影+RQ-VAE 将 CF 嵌入映射为冻结 LLM 词表中的离散 token，配合对比对齐实现免微调的语义可解释与性能增益。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;arXiv 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;保形预测&quot;,&quot;提示工程去偏&quot;,&quot;LLM推荐公平性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/facter_fairness_aware_conformal_thresholding_and_prompt_engineering_for_ena/">FACTER: Fairness-Aware Conformal Thresholding and Prompt Engineering for Enabling Fair LLM-Based Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用保形分位数阈值+违规触发提示工程，在黑盒 LLM 推荐器上闭环降低人口统计偏见，MovieLens 违规数降 95.5% 且精度基本不掉。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;逐步GRPO&quot;,&quot;势函数奖励塑形&quot;,&quot;列表级用药推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/llm_rec/fine_grained_list_wise_alignment_for_generative_medication_recommendation/">Fine-grained List-wise Alignment for Generative Medication Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将用药推荐重构为逐药序列决策，提出逐步GRPO与势函数奖励塑形实现细粒度信用分配，在MIMIC-III上Jaccard达0.4836并支持安全-精度可控权衡。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;arXiv&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;OOV Token&quot;,&quot;META ID&quot;,&quot;元路径聚类&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/llm_rec/improving_llms_for_recommendation_with_out_of_vocabulary_tokens/">Improving LLMs for Recommendation with Out-Of-Vocabulary Tokens</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用元路径聚类生成 OOV 层级 token 构建用户/物品 META ID，兼顾记忆性与多样性，序列推荐 HR@10 最高提升 30%+。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;语言表征同构&quot;,&quot;线性映射探针&quot;,&quot;零样本推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/llm_rec/language_representations_can_be_what_recommenders_need_findings_and_potenti/">Language Representations Can be What Recommenders Need: Findings and Potentials</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示语言空间与推荐行为空间同构，提出纯语言表征的 AlphaRec，在 Amazon 三数据集 Full-Ranking 上超越 ID-based CF 6.8%–9.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;可复现评估&quot;,&quot;隐私保护数据构建&quot;,&quot;网页推荐&quot;,&quot;LLM检索式推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/orbit_open_recommendation_benchmark_for_reproducible_research_with_hidden_t/">ORBIT: Open Recommendation Benchmark for Reproducible Research with Hidden Tests</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>统一可复现推荐基准，含5个公开数据集标准化划分与ClueWeb-Reco隐私安全网页推荐隐藏测试集，评测12个模型并引入LLM-QueryGen基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR/NeurIPS/ICML 2025&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端策略优化&quot;,&quot;Knowledge Tracing&quot;,&quot;Model-Based Value Estimation&quot;,&quot;语义表征学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/rl_bandit_rec/personalized_exercise_recommendation_with_semantically_grounded_knowledge_t/">Personalized Exercise Recommendation with Semantically-Grounded Knowledge Tracing</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>ExRec 用 LLM 自动标注知识点+对比学习语义表征+KT 校准，并以 KT 模型做 Model-Based Value Estimation 稳定 Q-learning，在四个教育推荐任务上显著提升知识状态增益。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;双头架构&quot;,&quot;强化学习联合优化&quot;,&quot;融合奖励&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/llm_rec/r_2_ec_towards_large_recommender_models_with_reasoning/">R$^2$ec: Towards Large Recommender Models with Reasoning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>双头架构统一推理生成与物品打分，RecPO 融合奖励 RL 联合优化，Full-Ranking 下 NDCG@10 最高提升 72%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;逐层特征对齐&quot;,&quot;序列推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/llm_rec/slmrec_distilling_large_language_models_into_small_for_sequential_recommend/">SLMRec: Distilling Large Language Models into Small for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>发现 LLM 中间层冗余，用逐层分块特征蒸馏将 8 层教师压缩为 4 层学生，仅 13% 参数达 6.6x/8.0x 加速且性能不降。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;GRPO&quot;,&quot;推理增强评分预测&quot;,&quot;规则奖励建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/llm_rec/think_before_recommendation_autonomous_reasoning_enhanced_recommender/">Think before Recommendation: Autonomous Reasoning-enhanced Recommender</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用纯 GRPO 强化学习替代教师蒸馏，让单个 LLM 自主习得「用户分析→物品分析→匹配→打分」推理链，评分预测 MAE 最高降 29.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;数据增强/训练数据侧&quot;,&quot;偏好树推理&quot;,&quot;多样性推荐&quot;,&quot;合成交互去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/llm_rec/tree_of_preferences_for_diversified_recommendation/">Tree of Preferences for Diversified Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 构建粗到细偏好树挖掘欠探索兴趣并合成交互去偏，在 Twitter/Weibo/Amazon 上同时提升多样性与相关性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;任务无关视觉历史&quot;,&quot;多模态大模型个性化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/multimodal_rec/visuallens_personalization_through_task_agnostic_visual_history/">VisualLens: Personalization through Task-Agnostic Visual History</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用任务无关的日常照片历史构建“图像-描述-方面词”谱系画像，经检索+网格化+联合微调实现个性化推荐，Hit@3 超 UniMP 5-10%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;召回/主动elicitation&quot;,&quot;AI Persona Prior&quot;,&quot;Bayesian Experimental Design&quot;,&quot;闭式后验更新&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/cold_start_rec/adaptive_querying_with_ai_persona_priors/">Adaptive Querying with AI Persona Priors</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 人物画像构建有限混合贝叶斯先验，实现闭式后验更新与自适应提问，在冷启动用户/题目场景下优于 CAT/IRT 基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;文本瓶颈&quot;,&quot;可控推荐&quot;,&quot;多模态内容理解&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/multimodal_rec/controllable_and_content_based_recommendations/">Controllable and Content-Based Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用多模态 LLM 将图像/音频/视频条目转述为文本画像，经标签监督与 InfoNCE 对齐冻结 CF 骨干，实现可读可编辑的文本瓶颈控制。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;参数掩码控制&quot;,&quot;请求感知推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/sequential_rec/cramer_control_via_request_aware_masking_for_editing_recommenders/">CRAMER: Control via Request-Aware Masking for Editing Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将自然语言请求编码为稀疏行列门控掩码，对冻结序列推荐骨干做后验行为编辑，采样100负例排序下全面超越四类请求感知基线且推理仅增0.018s。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双画像同质性门控&quot;,&quot;免训练反思更新&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/llm_rec/harp_training_free_dual_profile_agentic_communication_for_llm_based_recomme/">HARP: Training-Free Dual-Profile Agentic Communication for LLM-Based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 LLM 推荐重构为四阶段智能体通信，用双画像、同质性门控检索与单次反思三个免训练算子补齐缺失环节，Hit@1 相对提升 6.9%–24.0%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;工具增强推理&quot;,&quot;强化微调&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/multimodal_rec/intentlens_grounding_underspecified_multimodal_queries_for_recommendation_v/">IntentLens: Grounding Underspecified Multimodal Queries for Recommendation via Tool-Augmented Reasoning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 MLLM 工具增强推理将欠指定图文查询接地为结构化意图，再以轻量双塔排序，构建两个新基准并显著超越 MLLM 与检索基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;证据高亮&quot;,&quot;弱监督强化学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/llm_rec/learning_evidence_highlighting_for_frozen_llms/">Learning Evidence Highlighting for Frozen LLMs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用轻量 Emphasis Actor 以 RL 在冻结 LLM 的原始上下文中插入高亮标签，解耦证据选择与推理，序列推荐 HR@10 相对最优基线提升 5.38%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;偏好状态修复&quot;,&quot;冻结宿主适配&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/sequential_rec/not_all_is_lost_repairing_lossy_user_preference_states_of_personalization_e/">Not All Is Lost: Repairing Lossy User Preference States of Personalization Encoders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>冻结宿主编码器与任务头，仅训练轻量 REPAIR 模块，用缓存逐步表征对压缩偏好状态做时序结构化残差校正，12 个推荐宿主 MRR/nDCG@10 全面提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;合成数据课程&quot;,&quot;缩放定律&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/llm_rec/principled_synthetic_data_enables_the_first_scaling_laws_for_llms_in_recomm/">Principled Synthetic Data Enables the First Scaling Laws for LLMs in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用分层合成数据课程（Item-Text/CF/UIH）替代噪声交互日志，首次在 0.6B–8B LLM CPT 上拟合出推荐域幂律缩放律，SasRec 在合成数据上 Recall@100 提升 130%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;用户行为模拟&quot;,&quot;主动推荐&quot;,&quot;DPO偏好对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/agent_conversational_rec/propersim_developing_proactive_and_personalized_ai_assistants_through_user_/">ProPerSim: Developing Proactive and Personalized AI Assistants through User-Assistant Simulation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 ProPerSim 仿真任务与 ProPerAssistant 基线，用 LLM 用户智能体+四维评分准则+DPO 偏好学习，让助手在时机与内容上同时个性化，14 天内平均满意度从 2.2 升至 3.3/4。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;交叉熵损失&quot;,&quot;排序模仿&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/llm_rec/rejuvenating_cross_entropy_loss_in_knowledge_distillation_for_recommender_s/">Rejuvenating Cross-Entropy Loss in Knowledge Distillation for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示CE蒸馏损失与NDCG的闭包假设矛盾，提出师生协同子集拆分与自适应加权的RCE-KD，全库排序下NDCG@20最高提升11.76%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;视觉表征&quot;,&quot;检索式推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/llm_rec/token_efficient_item_representation_via_images_for_llm_recommender_systems/">Token-Efficient Item Representation via Images for LLM Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用单张物品图像替代冗长文本描述作为 LLM 推荐输入，配合 RISA 对齐与 RERI 检索式推荐，在 Amazon 四域上兼顾效率与效果。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信隐私&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;稀疏自编码器&quot;,&quot;叠加假设&quot;,&quot;可解释性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/user_embeddings_are_superpositions_of_interpretable_behavioral_modes/">User Embeddings are Superpositions of Interpretable Behavioral Modes</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将机制可解释性的叠加假设迁移到用户嵌入，用 SAE 事后分解出可命名行为模式，支撑零样本自然语言受众检索与画像。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排/端到端联邦个性化&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;低秩适配器(LoRA 式)&quot;,&quot;自适应门控融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/federated_adaptation_for_foundation_model_based_recommendations/">Federated Adaptation for Foundation Model-based Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 FedPA：以预训练双塔推荐模型为底座，客户端仅训练低秩适配器（用户级私有 + 用户组级共享）并由自适应门控融合公共知识与个性化，联邦优化中冻结物品/预测模块，AUC 最高相对提升 3.4%，可训练参数减少 40%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI-ECAI 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;基础模型个性化&quot;,&quot;语义隐私边界&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/a_survey_of_personalized_federated_foundation_models_for_privacy_preserving/">A Survey of Personalized Federated Foundation Models for Privacy-Preserving Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>综述提出“主动语义保人格”范式，用几何正交约束与语义感知聚合解耦全局知识与用户人格，并给出五类开放问题与未来路线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;提示学习 (Prompt Learning)&quot;,&quot;知识图谱语义融合&quot;,&quot;回复模板一致性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2022/agent_conversational_rec/towards_unified_conversational_recommender_systems_via_knowledge_enhanced_p/">Towards Unified Conversational Recommender Systems via Knowledge-Enhanced Prompt Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>冻结 DialoGPT，用知识增强软提示统一推荐与回复生成，并以回复模板回流推荐，ReDial R@10 相对提升 26.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;预训练语言模型加速&quot;,&quot;训练框架优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2022/llm_rec/training_large_scale_news_recommenders_with_pretrained_language_models_in_t/">Training Large-Scale News Recommenders with Pretrained Language Models in the Loop</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>SpeedyFeed 用集中式编码、缓存复用、BusLM 与自回归用户建模，把 PLM 新闻推荐训练加速 128.7×，AUC 73.74 全面超越 NRMS。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;Gen-RecSys 分类学&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec 与 RAG&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/generative_rec/a_review_of_modern_recommender_systems_using_generative_models_gen_recsys/">A Review of Modern Recommender Systems Using Generative Models (Gen-RecSys)</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>按数据模态（交互/文本/多模态）×生成范式（AE/AR/GAN/Diffusion/LLM）构建 Gen-RecSys 二维分类学，并系统梳理评估协议与社会危害框架。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)&quot;,&quot;生成式端到端&quot;,&quot;多侧面物品标识&quot;,&quot;受限解码&quot;,&quot;生成接地&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/llm_rec/bridging_items_and_language_a_transition_paradigm_for_large_language_model_/">Bridging Items and Language: A Transition Paradigm for Large Language Model-Based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>多侧面标识(ID+标题+属性)+FM-index 位置无关受限生成+聚合接地，全库排序下 Yelp R@5 提升 33.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;对抗攻击&quot;,&quot;LLM Agent&quot;,&quot;Prompt Tuning&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/cheatagent_attacking_llm_empowered_recommender_systems_via_llm_agent/">CheatAgent: Attacking LLM-Empowered Recommender Systems via LLM Agent</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用掩码定位插入位 + LLM 智能体生成对抗扰动并以前缀提示自反思优化，在黑盒下显著击穿 P5/TALLRec 推荐系统。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;多模态大模型适配&quot;,&quot;AIGC 个性化生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/multimodal_rec/multimodal_pretraining_adaptation_and_generation_for_recommendation_a_surve/">Multimodal Pretraining, Adaptation, and Generation for Recommendation: A Survey</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以预训练-适配-生成三轴重构多模态推荐文献地图，剖析语义空间与行为空间的错配矛盾，并指出统一评测与推荐基础模型的空白。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Policy Gradient 大动作空间&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-Reward / Novelty 优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/rl_bandit_rec/optimizing_novelty_of_top_k_recommendations_using_large_language_models_and/">Optimizing Novelty of Top-k Recommendations using Large Language Models and Reinforcement Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 top-k 新颖性优化重构为 ⟨query,item⟩ 二值动作策略梯度，用 GPT-3.5 作奖励模型，把样本复杂度从 $A^2$降到$A$，工业搜索引擎 Novelty@200 提升 36%、A/B 点击率 +0.26%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;模型可解释性&quot;,&quot;代理模型对齐&quot;,&quot;多模态嵌入对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/llm_rec/recexplainer_aligning_large_language_models_for_explaining_recommendation_m/">RecExplainer: Aligning Large Language Models for Explaining Recommendation Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用行为/意图/混合三种对齐把 LLM 训练成黑盒双塔推荐模型的替身解释器，并在三数据集上验证保真度与解释质量</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;特征工程/数据侧&quot;,&quot;LLM Agent&quot;,&quot;多值类别特征&quot;,&quot;AutoML 闭环反馈&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/llm_rec/agent0_leveraging_llm_agents_to_discover_multi_value_features_from_text_for/">Agent0: Leveraging LLM Agents to Discover Multi-value Features from Text for Enhanced Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Architect-Sentinel-Oracle 三角色 LLM 智能体闭环自动挖掘文本多值类别特征，以 DCNv2+RIG 为 Oracle 在真实广告生产数据上取得 0.005+ RIG 提升并提速 2-3 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLM 自动构图&quot;,&quot;残差向量量化&quot;,&quot;元路径消息传播&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/graph_rec/an_automatic_graph_construction_framework_based_on_large_language_models_fo/">An Automatic Graph Construction Framework based on Large Language Models for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 逐点生成语义向量 + 残差量化抽取隐因子作额外节点，O(N) 构图并设计元路径聚合，Full-Ranking 下 NDCG@10 最高提升 43.57%，华为广告 A/B 提升 RPM 2.69%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;端云协同&quot;,&quot;协同训练&quot;,&quot;一致性触发请求&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/llm_rec/collaboration_of_large_language_models_and_small_recommendation_models_for_/">Collaboration of Large Language Models and Small Recommendation Models for Device-Cloud Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出端云协同 LSC4Rec：云端 LLM 召回+设备端 SRM 实时重排，配合协同训练、分数融合与不一致度触发请求，Beauty 平均提升 16.18%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;KV Cache 压缩&quot;,&quot;寄存器 Token&quot;,&quot;推理加速&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/llm_rec/earn_efficient_inference_acceleration_for_llm_based_generative_recommendati/">EARN: Efficient Inference Acceleration for LLM-based Generative Recommendation by Register Tokens</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>基于 LLMRec 注意力层间稀疏反转与双注意力汇现象，用首尾寄存器 token 压缩历史交互并在第 k 层后剪除 prompt，实现 3.79× 加速与 80.8% KV Cache 缩减且精度反超微调。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;图信号处理&quot;,&quot;频域重加权&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/llm_rec/exploring_feature_based_knowledge_distillation_for_recommender_system_a_fre/">Exploring Feature-based Knowledge Distillation for Recommender System: A Frequency Perspective</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>从频域视角揭示特征蒸馏等权缺陷，提出轻量图滤波重加权 FreqD，全库排序下 Recall@20 最高提升 18.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;数据侧优化/核心集选择&quot;,&quot;最优传输&quot;,&quot;梯度范数界&quot;,&quot;组级选择&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/llm_rec/goracs_group_level_optimal_transport_guided_coreset_selection_for_llm_based/">GORACS: Group-level Optimal Transport-guided Coreset Selection for LLM-based Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用最优传输+梯度范数构造测试损失上界代理目标，配合贪心初始化-交换精炼两阶段算法做组级核心集选择，20%训练开销超越全量微调。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Trie 约束解码&quot;,&quot;查询生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/generative_rec/great_guiding_query_generation_with_a_trie_for_recommending_related_search_/">GREAT: Guiding Query Generation with a Trie for Recommending Related Search about Video at Kuaishou</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用高质量查询构建 Trie 约束 LLM 训练与解码，实现短视频到查询的 I2Q 推荐，离线 Edit@1 最优、线上 CTR 与相关性双升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;画像生成/端到端&quot;,&quot;DPO偏好对齐&quot;,&quot;开放式用户画像&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/llm_rec/lettingo_explore_user_profile_generation_for_recommendation_system/">LettinGo: Explore User Profile Generation for Recommendation System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用多LLM探索开放式用户画像、以下游推荐成败构造偏好对、DPO对齐画像生成器，Amazon Books Acc 66.3%→70.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;协同监督微调&quot;,&quot;物品级嵌入建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/llm_rec/llm2rec_large_language_models_are_powerful_embedding_models_for_sequential_/">LLM2Rec: Large Language Models Are Powerful Embedding Models for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>两阶段将 LLM 改造为融合协同信号与语义的物品嵌入模型，Full-Ranking 下域内平均提升约 15%、跨域仍稳定增益。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;序列信息蒸馏&quot;,&quot;表示均匀性正则&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/llm_rec/lost_in_sequence_do_large_language_models_understand_sequential_recommendat/">Lost in Sequence: Do Large Language Models Understand Sequential Recommendation?</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示现有 LLM4Rec 模型无法捕捉交互序列顺序信息，提出冻结 LLM 与 CF-SRec、仅训练轻量 MLP 的蒸馏框架 LLM-SRec，在 Amazon 四数据集上 NDCG@10 全面超越 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多模态大表征模型&quot;,&quot;后期融合&quot;,&quot;多模态上下文学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/multimodal_rec/notellm_2_multimodal_large_representation_models_for_recommendation/">NoteLLM-2: Multimodal Large Representation Models for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>端到端微调 LLM+视觉编码器构建高效 MLRM，用 mICL 与门控后期融合缓解视觉忽视，I2I 全库召回 R@100 显著提升并落地线上 A/B。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;I2I Retrieval&quot;,&quot;Multi-Task Learning&quot;,&quot;Knowledge Distillation&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/collaborative_filtering/optimizing_recall_or_relevance_a_multi_task_multi_head_approach_for_item_to/">Optimizing Recall or Relevance? A Multi-Task Multi-Head Approach for Item-to-Item Retrieval in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用多任务蒸馏损失+多头I2I架构联合优化共互动召回与语义相关性，离线召回@500提升14.4%、L2主题相关性提升56.5%，并完成线上A/B验证。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;心智世界&quot;,&quot;多模态知识推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/llm_rec/the_mental_world_of_large_language_models_in_recommendation_a_benchmark_on_/">The Mental World of Large Language Models in Recommendation: A Benchmark on Association, Personalization, and Knowledgeability</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 LRWorld 基准（38K 样本/23M tokens），从关联、个性化、知识性三尺度十因素 31 任务系统评测数十个 LLM，揭示其在神经嵌入检索上的致命短板。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;用户嵌入软提示&quot;,&quot;个性化问答基准&quot;,&quot;缩放定律&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/llm_rec/uqabench_evaluating_user_embedding_for_prompting_llms_in_personalized_quest/">UQABench: Evaluating User Embedding for Prompting LLMs in Personalized Question Answering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出首个标准化三阶段（预训练-微调-评测）用户嵌入软提示基准，用序列理解/行为预测/兴趣感知三类任务揭示嵌入式生成推荐与文本式相当且省 8-19× token。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;原子意图缓存&quot;,&quot;目标感知意图检索&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/cross_domain_multi_scenario/atomic_intent_reasoning_bringing_llm_semantics_to_industrial_cross_domain_r/">Atomic Intent Reasoning: Bringing LLM Semantics to Industrial Cross-Domain Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 LLM 意图推理离线化为原子行为-意图对缓存，在线经意图树构建与目标感知检索实现约 400× 加速，快手 A/B 测试 GMV +3.446%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;LLM推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;方面解耦&quot;,&quot;多重图消息传递&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/llm_rec/copersona_collaborative_persona_graphs_for_robust_llm_personalization/">CoPersona: Collaborative Persona Graphs for Robust LLM Personalization</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用方面级多重人物图+双分支推理补全稀疏用户画像，Amazon六域生成式评测全面超越DEP/DPL。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Attention Sink&quot;,&quot;语言模型CTR&quot;,&quot;行为序列建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/ctr_feature_interaction/ctr_sink_attention_sink_for_language_models_in_click_through_rate_predictio/">CTR-Sink: Attention Sink for Language Models in Click-Through Rate Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在文本化行为序列中插入携带时间距离的 [SINK] 锚点并配合两阶段训练与 sink 间注意力增强，缓解语义碎片化，AUC 稳定提升 0.2–0.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;语义画像生成&quot;,&quot;知识图谱传播&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/llm_rec/enriching_semantic_profiles_into_knowledge_graph_for_recommender_systems_us/">Enriching Semantic Profiles into Knowledge Graph for Recommender Systems Using Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 为 KG 全实体生成语义画像，以可逆加性注入+成对匹配对齐，Full-Ranking 下 Recall@40 最高提升 2.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Agent Memory&quot;,&quot;Hierarchical Retrieval&quot;,&quot;RAG&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/agent_conversational_rec/hierarchical_long_term_semantic_memory_for_linkedin_s_hiring_agent/">Hierarchical Long-Term Semantic Memory for LinkedIn&#x27;s Hiring Agent</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>HLTM 用 schema 对齐的层级记忆树 + 多视图表示 + 无损增量索引，为 LinkedIn 招聘智能体实现低延迟、隐私隔离的长期个性化记忆，正确率提升 5%+、检索 F1 提升 10%+。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026 TSMO&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;隐式意图生成&quot;,&quot;教师学生蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/llm_rec/improving_item_discoverability_in_e_commerce_search_via_related_intent_gene/">Improving Item Discoverability in e-Commerce Search via Related Intent Generation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用闭源LLM+蒸馏SLM生成替代/互补/主题三类隐式意图以扩展电商搜索召回，会话购买预测与LLM评审双轨评估，覆盖流量60%→80%、成本仅30%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;离线评测&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-a-Judge&quot;,&quot;语义代理&quot;,&quot;MNAR 去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/llm_as_a_judge_for_reliable_and_explainable_offline_evaluation_in_top_k_rec/">LLM-as-a-Judge for Reliable and Explainable Offline Evaluation in Top-K Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 从用户文本行为构建语义偏好代理替代 holdout ID 匹配，以 reasoning-then-scoring 生成可解释 Top-K 评测，模型排序一致性较常规测试提升 40–150%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;多轮 Grounding&quot;,&quot;GRPO 强化学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/llm_rec/mgfrec_towards_reinforced_reasoning_recommendation_with_multiple_groundings/">MGFRec: Towards Reinforced Reasoning Recommendation with Multiple Groundings and Feedback</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将单步 grounding 扩展为多轮物品空间接地并引入用户 Agent 反馈，用 GRPO 训练 LLM 推理推荐，Full-Ranking 下 H@5 最高提升约 8.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;KG-RAG&quot;,&quot;MoE&quot;,&quot;多智能体强化学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/llm_rec/mixture_of_experts_knowledge_graph_retrieval_augmented_generation_for_multi/">Mixture-of-Experts Knowledge Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Multi-Agent LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 MoE 多粒度 KG 检索智能体按查询复杂度路由检索粒度，配合知识对齐与对比偏好优化，在 20 候选池协议下 Accuracy 相对提升 5.8%–20.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;画像外推&quot;,&quot;不变性学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/llm_rec/proex_a_unified_framework_leveraging_large_language_model_with_profile_extr/">ProEx: A Unified Framework Leveraging Large Language Model with Profile Extrapolation for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用四步 CoT 为每个用户/物品生成多面画像并做环境不变性融合，在三个数据集上对 6 个基模型平均提升 5%+。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;语义ID&quot;,&quot;GRPO 强化学习&quot;,&quot;SID-语言对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/generative_rec/reasoning_over_semantic_ids_enhances_generative_recommendation/">Reasoning over Semantic IDs Enhances Generative Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>通过教师模型合成富化语料强化 SID-语言对齐，再用 GRPO 结果驱动强化学习激发 SID 推理，三数据集 Full-Ranking 全面超越 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双塔稠密检索&quot;,&quot;Matryoshka 表征学习&quot;,&quot;多语言多垂类&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/collaborative_filtering/scaling_multilingual_semantic_search_in_uber_eats_delivery/">Scaling Multilingual Semantic Search in Uber Eats Delivery</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>基于 gte-Qwen2 双塔统一多语言多垂类语义召回，InfoNCE+Triplet NCE 两阶段训练与 MRL 多维度服务，Store R@20 相对 Legacy 提升 88%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;模型合并&quot;,&quot;锐度感知优化&quot;,&quot;LoRA 重参数化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/cross_domain_multi_scenario/sharpness_aware_model_merging_with_salience_recovery_for_llm_based_cross_do/">Sharpness-aware Model Merging with Salience Recovery for LLM-based Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用锐度感知平坦极小值对齐 + 重尾重参数化恢复显著偏好信号，破解 LLM 跨域序列推荐模型合并中的参数冲突与多域饱和瓶颈。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Hybrid-Query Connector&quot;,&quot;可靠性门控&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/multimodal_rec/text_guided_visual_representation_learning_for_robust_multimodal_e_commerce/">Text-Guided Visual Representation Learning for Robust Multimodal E-Commerce Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以结构化元数据初始化语义查询、双流解耦视觉证据并用可靠性双门控校准，在 134K 全库 I2I 检索上 H@100 相对提升 6.04%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;不确定性建模&quot;,&quot;GRPO偏好优化&quot;,&quot;置信度对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/generative_rec/uncertainty_aware_generative_recommendation/">Uncertainty-aware Generative Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将生成置信度与样本难度转化为GRPO奖励与梯度权重，并显式对齐置信度token，稳定训练并支持风险感知推荐。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;基准审计&quot;,&quot;推理期干预&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/llm_rec/whose_name_comes_up_ii_benchmarking_and_intervention_based_auditing_of_llm_/">Whose Name Comes Up? II: Benchmarking and Intervention-Based Auditing of LLM-Based Scholar Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 LLMScholarBench，联合审计 22 个 LLM 在 5 类学者推荐任务、3 类推理期干预下的技术质量与社会代表性权衡。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2023&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Genre Spectrum&quot;,&quot;内容元数据嵌入&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/llm_rec/beyond_labels_leveraging_deep_learning_and_llms_for_content_metadata/">Beyond Labels: Leveraging Deep Learning and LLMs for Content Metadata</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 Genre Spectrum，用多标签监督把离散类型标签映射到连续隐空间，Mixup 增广后于 1.1M 影视数据 top-100 类型相似度低热度桶提升约 12 个百分点。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2023&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/特征增强&quot;,&quot;异构行为融合&quot;,&quot;指令微调 LoRA&quot;,&quot;自然语言用户画像&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/llm_rec/heterogeneous_knowledge_fusion_a_novel_approach_for_personalized_recommenda/">Heterogeneous Knowledge Fusion: A Novel Approach for Personalized Recommendation via LLM</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 ChatGPT 将美团外卖多维异构行为改写为自然语言知识，再以 LoRA 指令微调 ChatGLM-6B 输出类别/POI 偏好，离线 HR@5 提升 52.5%，冷启动在线 CTR +2.45%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2023&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;合成查询数据增强&quot;,&quot;叙事驱动推荐&quot;,&quot;双塔检索&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/llm_rec/large_language_model_augmented_narrative_driven_recommendations/">Large Language Model Augmented Narrative Driven Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 175B LLM 从 Yelp 评论合成叙事查询，训练 110M 双塔/交叉编码器，Pointrec 上 NDCG@10 相对提升 7%+。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/精排&quot;,&quot;LLM item embedding&quot;,&quot;PCA initialization&quot;,&quot;BERT4Rec&quot;,&quot;prompt fine-tuning&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/sequential_rec/leveraging_large_language_models_for_sequential_recommendation/">Leveraging Large Language Models for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>三种LLM增强序列推荐：LLM物品嵌入+PCA初始化BERT4Rec在Beauty/Delivery Hero上NDCG@20提升15–20%，简单语义相似检索在Beauty具竞争力。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;贝叶斯优化&quot;,&quot;自然语言偏好引导&quot;,&quot;Thompson Sampling&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/agent_conversational_rec/bayesian_optimization_with_llm_based_acquisition_functions_for_natural_lang/">Bayesian Optimization with LLM-Based Acquisition Functions for Natural Language Preference Elicitation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将自然语言偏好引导建模为贝叶斯优化，用 NLI 蕴含分维护 Beta 效用后验，并以 TS/UCB 指导 LLM 生成查询；10 轮对话后 Yelp MRR@10 达 0.27，超最优单体 LLM 基线 58.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式召回&quot;,&quot;生成式 LLM 微调&quot;,&quot;对比对齐&quot;,&quot;BM25 文本检索&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/sequential_rec/calrec_contrastive_alignment_of_generative_llms_for_sequential_recommendati/">CALRec: Contrastive Alignment of Generative LLMs for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>两阶段微调生成式 LLM，双塔对比对齐 + 文本生成损失，Amazon 5-core 全库排序 Recall@1 提升 37.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;序列推荐&quot;,&quot;排序蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/llm_rec/distillation_matters_empowering_sequential_recommenders_to_match_the_perfor/">Distillation Matters: Empowering Sequential Recommenders to Match the Performance of Large Language Model</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 BIGRec 教师蒸馏至轻量序列模型：置信/一致性加权排序蒸馏 + projector-offset 嵌入蒸馏，平均提升 47.97%，推理降至秒级。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双塔离线预计算&quot;,&quot;Poly-Attention 多向量表征&quot;,&quot;LLM 摘要蒸馏监督&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/llm_rec/embsum_leveraging_the_summarization_capabilities_of_large_language_models_f/">EmbSum: Leveraging the Summarization Capabilities of Large Language Models for Content-Based Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 T5 编码器-解码器 + Poly-Attention 生成用户/内容多向量表征，并以 LLM 摘要作离线监督，双塔离线预计算下 MIND AUC 71.95。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;跨模态细粒度对齐&quot;,&quot;掩码重建预训练&quot;,&quot;PLM 增强推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/ctr_feature_interaction/flip_fine_grained_alignment_between_id_based_models_and_pretrained_language/">FLIP: Fine-grained Alignment between ID-based Models and Pretrained Language Models for CTR Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以字段级双向掩码重建实现 ID 与词元细粒度对齐，MovieLens-1M 上 AUC 0.8621、较最强基线相对提升 0.57%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;离线评测&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-a-Judge&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;元评估协议&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/llm_rec/large_language_models_as_evaluators_for_recommendation_explanations/">Large Language Models as Evaluators for Recommendation Explanations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以真实用户四维 Likert 评分为真值，提出三级元评估协议验证 GPT-4 零样本/单样本/异构集成可替代第三方标注与 BLEU/ROUGE 评估推荐解释文本，成本仅约 1/4。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;CTR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;实时场景语义理解&quot;,&quot;跨模态对比对齐&quot;,&quot;LLM 离线推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/ctr_feature_interaction/larr_large_language_model_aided_real_time_scene_recommendation_with_semanti/">LARR: Large Language Model Aided Real-time Scene Recommendation with Semantic Understanding</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>三阶段将 LLM 转为 POI/用户文本嵌入器，用双向编码器聚合实时场景语义并与协同信号对齐，离线化推理使美团外卖 CTR AUC +0.58%，线上 CTR +2.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;用户兴趣探索&quot;,&quot;混合分层规划&quot;,&quot;受控生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/llm_rec/llms_for_user_interest_exploration_in_large_scale_recommendation_systems/">LLMs for User Interest Exploration in Large-scale Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 在 761 个兴趣簇空间内生成新颖兴趣、再用受限 softmax 落回物品级序列推荐，离线枚举查表支撑线上服务，活体实验同时提升新颖曝光与正反馈。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;PLM 参数冗余&quot;,&quot;行为微调初始化&quot;,&quot;层间注意力分层&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/sequential_rec/the_elephant_in_the_room_rethinking_the_usage_of_pre_trained_language_model/">The Elephant in the Room: Rethinking the Usage of Pre-trained Language Model in Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 PLM 行为序列建模的参数冗余，改用行为微调 PLM 仅做物品嵌入初始化，配合轻量 ID 序列模型大幅提升且零额外推理开销</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;对话推荐（推荐+回复生成）&quot;,&quot;情感感知偏好建模&quot;,&quot;检索增强生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/agent_conversational_rec/towards_empathetic_conversational_recommender_systems/">Towards Empathetic Conversational Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出ECR，以情感感知实体与反馈重加权提升推荐，并用检索增强生成情感回复；ReDial全库AUC+6.9%、RT@10+3.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因子化提示&quot;,&quot;混合专家适配器&quot;,&quot;知识增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/llm_rec/towards_open_world_recommendation_with_knowledge_augmentation_from_large_la/">Towards Open-World Recommendation with Knowledge Augmentation from Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用因子化提示从 LLM 抽取用户偏好推理知识与物品事实知识，经混合专家适配器压缩为增强向量注入任意精排模型，MovieLens/Amazon 上 AUC 提升 1%+，在线新闻召回 +7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;RAG&quot;,&quot;模型更新策略&quot;,&quot;兴趣探索&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/llm_rec/balancing_fine_tuning_and_rag_a_hybrid_strategy_for_dynamic_llm_recommendat/">Balancing Fine-tuning and RAG: A Hybrid Strategy for Dynamic LLM Recommendation Updates</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以十亿用户短视频兴趣探索系统为案例，量化对比微调与RAG的更新代价/敏捷性，提出月度微调+周内RAG刷新的混合更新策略，线上A/B显著提升满意度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;画像生成&quot;,&quot;用户研究&quot;,&quot;因果去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/llm_rec/biases_in_llm_generated_musical_taste_profiles_for_recommendation/">Biases in LLM-Generated Musical Taste Profiles for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>通过 64 人用户研究揭示 LLM 生成音乐品味画像存在流派/国别系统性偏差，且用户认同度与下游推荐指标仅弱相关。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;通用文本嵌入&quot;,&quot;零样本迁移&quot;,&quot;有效维度&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/cold_start_rec/do_we_really_need_specialization_evaluating_generalist_text_embeddings_for_/">Do We Really Need Specialization? Evaluating Generalist Text Embeddings for Zero-Shot Recommendation and Search</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>实证通用文本嵌入(GTE)零样本即超越领域微调模型，并以PCA有效维度揭示空间利用率与压缩收益。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;离线评测&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-a-Judge&quot;,&quot;用户画像&quot;,&quot;播客推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/llm_rec/evaluating_podcast_recommendations_with_profile_aware_llm_as_a_judge/">Evaluating Podcast Recommendations with Profile-Aware LLM-as-a-Judge</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用90天收听历史蒸馏自然语言用户画像，驱动零样本LLM做剧集级与模型级离线评判，47人用户研究中与人工判断高度一致。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;评测与元评估&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-Evaluator&quot;,&quot;Serendipity&quot;,&quot;用户模拟&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/cold_start_rec/exploring_the_potential_of_llms_for_serendipity_evaluation_in_recommender_s/">Exploring the Potential of LLMs for Serendipity Evaluation in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 模拟用户对推荐结果做惊喜度（serendipity）打分，在淘宝与 MovieLens 两个真实用户调研数据集上做元评估，最优配置 Pearson 相关达 21.5%，显著超越传统代理指标。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异构用户建模&quot;,&quot;域跷跷板&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/llm_rec/heterogeneous_user_modeling_for_llm_based_recommendation/">Heterogeneous User Modeling for LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用压缩提示+[USER]锚点token将跨域异构行为压成单一用户表征，并以域重要性平滑加权缓解域跷跷板，六域全库排序R@10最高提升约30%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;实体引导兴趣建模&quot;,&quot;对比预训练&quot;,&quot;双塔用户编码器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/collaborative_filtering/ip2_entity_guided_interest_probing_for_personalized_news_recommendation/">IP2: Entity-Guided Interest Probing for Personalized News Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以实体-标题对比预训练探测新闻内实体焦点，并用跨注意力双塔用户编码器建模新闻间实体引导兴趣，MIND-large AUC 达 72.06%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;TransE 平移建模&quot;,&quot;多模态交叉注意力&quot;,&quot;自然语言查询推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/multimodal_rec/just_ask_for_music_jam_multimodal_and_personalized_natural_language_music_r/">Just Ask for Music (JAM): Multimodal and Personalized Natural Language Music Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将查询建模为用户到物品的向量平移，用交叉注意力聚合音频/歌词/CF 多模态，在 JAMSessions 全库排序上 NDCG@10 相对 TalkRec 提升约 105%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-Annotator&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;个性化受限召回&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/multimodal_rec/llm_powered_nuanced_video_attribute_annotation_for_enhanced_recommendations/">LLM-Powered Nuanced Video Attribute Annotation for Enhanced Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Gemini 多模态 LLM 做短视频“氛围”属性标注，经金/银集评估与蒸馏扩量，接入个性化受限召回，线上参与度 +0.49%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;反事实解释&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;LLM 画像过滤&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/mitigating_popularity_bias_in_counterfactual_explanations_using_large_langu/">Mitigating Popularity Bias in Counterfactual Explanations using Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 生成用户画像并贪心剔除“出格”历史项，作为 ACCENT 反事实解释的即插即用预处理，使解释流行度分布更贴合用户偏好。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;LLM 反思 (Reflection)&quot;,&quot;Contextual Bandit 视角选择&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/sequential_rec/more_a_mixture_of_reflectors_framework_for_large_language_model_based_seque/">MoRE: A Mixture of Reflectors Framework for Large Language Model-Based Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用显式/隐式/协同三视角离线反思 + 元反思器自改进与 Contextual Bandit 在线选视角，LLM 序列推荐全库候选下 NDCG@10 最高提升 10.1%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;时间间隔建模&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec&quot;,&quot;Interval-Infused Attention&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/sequential_rec/not_just_what_but_when_integrating_irregular_intervals_to_llm_for_sequentia/">Not Just What, But When: Integrating Irregular Intervals to LLM for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将不规则时间间隔经 MLP 编码与 Interval-Infused Attention 注入 LLaMA-2，配合选项化提示，在 3 个 Amazon 数据集上 HR@1 平均提升 4.4%，并提出“间隔冷启动”新评测视角。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;模型反演攻击&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec 隐私&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/privacy_risks_of_llm_empowered_recommender_systems_an_inversion_attack_pers/">Privacy Risks of LLM-Empowered Recommender Systems: An Inversion Attack Perspective</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次系统研究 LLM 推荐系统的 logits 反演攻击，提出相似度引导精炼，可恢复 65% 交互物品与 87% 年龄性别。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;System-2 反思精炼&quot;,&quot;LLM 知识增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/ctr_feature_interaction/r4ec_a_reasoning_reflection_and_refinement_framework_for_recommendation_sys/">R4ec: A Reasoning, Reflection, and Refinement Framework for Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 actor/reflection 双小模型迭代反思精炼 LLM 知识，注入 CTR 主干，AUC 相对提升 2.5%+，线上收入 +2.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;语义ID&quot;,&quot;联合搜索推荐&quot;,&quot;RQ-KMeans&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/generative_rec/semantic_ids_for_joint_generative_search_and_recommendation/">Semantic IDs for Joint Generative Search and Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统对比任务专属与跨任务语义ID构建策略，发现多任务双塔嵌入+统一RQ-KMeans语义ID在联合搜索推荐中取得最佳权衡。</p>
</dd>
