<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;出价与预算分配&quot;,&quot;LLM 上下文学习&quot;,&quot;GRPO 微调&quot;,&quot;双阶段智能体&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/rl_bandit_rec/dara_few_shot_budget_allocation_in_online_advertising_via_in_context_decisi/">DARA: Few-shot Budget Allocation in Online Advertising via In-Context Decision Making with RL-Finetuned LLMs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>双阶段 LLM 智能体分离少样本规划与精细优化，配合动态参考策略 GRPO-Adaptive 微调，在真实与合成广告环境中显著降低边际 ROI 方差。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;连续Token分词&quot;,&quot;条件扩散生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/generative_rec/diffusion_generative_recommendation_with_continuous_tokens/">Diffusion Generative Recommendation with Continuous Tokens</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 σ-VAE 连续分词器替代量化码本，并以条件扩散+离散散度损失生成用户偏好，四数据集 Full-Ranking 上 HR@10 平均超 TIGER 11.76%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;联邦学习&quot;,&quot;LoRA 方向分解&quot;,&quot;个性化聚合权重&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/cross_domain_multi_scenario/fedecider_an_llm_based_framework_for_federated_cross_domain_recommendation/">FeDecider: An LLM-Based Framework for Federated Cross-Domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>联邦跨域 LLM 推荐中，服务器只广播 LoRA 更新的方向分量以消除尺度噪声，客户端用可学习个性化权重做数据感知融合，Goodreads 平均 Hit@5 提升 3.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;推理蒸馏&quot;,&quot;对比自蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/llm_rec/from_reasoning_llms_to_bert_a_two_stage_distillation_framework_for_search_r/">From Reasoning LLMs to BERT: A Two-Stage Distillation Framework for Search Relevance</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>美团搜索广告两阶段框架：领域自适应推理LLM做教师标注，CRSD自蒸馏将推理能力内化进6层BERT，线上AdCTR+0.91%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLM 语义嵌入&quot;,&quot;行为校准&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/sequential_rec/generative_archetype_grounded_item_representations_for_sequential_recommend/">Generative Archetype-Grounded Item Representations for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 生成物品“目标人群原型”文本并单次前向抽取 prefill/decoding 双嵌入，再以行为关联分数调制排斥力做校准，Full-Ranking 下 HR@10 最高 +6.95%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;时间间隔预测&quot;,&quot;上下文提示&quot;,&quot;零样本推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/llm_rec/is_more_context_always_better_examining_llm_reasoning_capability_for_time_i/">Is More Context Always Better? Examining LLM Reasoning Capability for Time Interval Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统评测 GPT-4o/Claude/Gemini 在复购时间间隔预测上的零样本能力，发现 LLM 仅胜统计基线、全面落后 XGBoost，且中等上下文最优、高上下文反降。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;语义推理&quot;,&quot;群体隐式兴趣&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/llm_rec/iterative_semantic_reasoning_from_individual_to_group_interests_for_generat/">Iterative Semantic Reasoning from Individual to Group Interests for Generative Recommendation with LLMs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 双向 CoT 推理物品/用户语义，构建个体显式兴趣图与相似用户群隐式兴趣图，并以对比蒸馏迭代对齐，直接推荐 H@10 最高提升 24.57%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;LLM Reasoning&quot;,&quot;GRPO&quot;,&quot;Cold-Start&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/cold_start_rec/llm_reasoning_for_cold_start_item_recommendation/">LLM Reasoning for Cold-Start Item Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在 Netflix 冷启动重排任务上系统对比结构化推理、软自洽与 SFT/GRPO 混合微调，推理微调模型在 Discovery 指标上超越线上生产排序模型最高 8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;LLM多智能体协作&quot;,&quot;动态编排&quot;,&quot;协同过滤&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/agent_conversational_rec/multi_agent_collaborative_filtering_orchestrating_users_and_items_for_agent/">Multi-Agent Collaborative Filtering: Orchestrating Users and Items for Agentic Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将相似用户与相关物品实例化为LLM智能体，由中心编排器动态招募并下发个性化指令，多轮讨论聚合协同信号，三域HR@10最高提升17.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;联邦持续个性化&quot;,&quot;软提示与原型锚定&quot;,&quot;差分隐私&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/prototype_aligned_federated_soft_prompts_for_continual_web_personalization/">Prototype-Aligned Federated Soft-Prompts for Continual Web Personalization</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>冻结骨干上注入双时间尺度软提示，长期提示锚定DP联邦原型库，八基准NDCG@10平均+2.9%且降低遗忘与适应步数。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;偏好漂移&quot;,&quot;区域级知识编辑&quot;,&quot;区域专属 LoRA&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/llm_rec/raie_region_aware_incremental_preference_editing_with_lora_for_llm_based_re/">RAIE: Region-Aware Incremental Preference Editing with LoRA for LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>冻结 LLM 主干，用球面 k-means 构建偏好区域并为每区配专属 LoRA，通过 Update/Expand/Add 三种区域级编辑与置信路由实现抗遗忘的增量偏好适配。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;用户模拟&quot;,&quot;指令遵循推荐&quot;,&quot;多轮强化学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/agent_conversational_rec/recoworld_building_simulated_environments_for_agentic_recommender_systems/">RecoWorld: Building Simulated Environments for Agentic Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出双视角LLM仿真环境，用户模拟器在多轮交互中发出反思指令，驱动指令遵循推荐器优化留存，并给出三种历史建模方案与多智能体群体仿真蓝图。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;规则感知奖励塑形&quot;,&quot;自适应引导回放&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/llm_rec/taosr_agrl_adaptive_guided_reinforcement_learning_framework_for_e_commerce_/">TaoSR-AGRL: Adaptive Guided Reinforcement Learning Framework for E-commerce Search Relevance</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用规则感知稠密奖励塑形与自适应引导回放缓解 GRPO 奖励稀疏，淘宝搜索相关性 Macro-F1 提升 2.28pt 并上线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;思维链蒸馏&quot;,&quot;LoRA 专家融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/llm_rec/thinkrec_thinking_based_recommendation_via_llm/">ThinkRec: Thinking-based Recommendation via LLM</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以合成推理链激活 LLM 的 System 2 思维，并用实例级 LoRA 专家融合适配异构用户，AUC 平均提升 7.96%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;协同信号对齐&quot;,&quot;软标签NTP&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/llm_rec/token_level_collaborative_alignment_for_llm_based_generative_recommendation/">Token-level Collaborative Alignment for LLM-based Generative Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将CF物品级logits投影为token级分布并与one-hot融合成软标签，以soft-NTP损失显式对齐LLM生成，即插即用提升多骨干推荐效果。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;语义ID&quot;,&quot;强化学习推理&quot;,&quot;电商搜索&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/generative_rec/towards_context_aware_reasoning_enhanced_generative_searching_in_e_commerce/">Towards Context-aware Reasoning-enhanced Generative Searching in E-commerce</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将电商搜索异构上下文统一为JSON文本与残差K-Means语义ID，用SFT-RL自演化后训练与去偏R-GRPO提升生成式搜索排序，All-100K上HR@10相对提升6.24%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Machine Unlearning&quot;,&quot;Influence Function&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec Fairness&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/towards_fair_large_language_model_based_recommender_systems_without_costly_/">Towards Fair Large Language Model-based Recommender Systems without Costly Retraining</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 LLM-RS 去偏重构为免重训机器遗忘任务：可学习掩码定位致偏样本 + 影响函数一步参数修正，公平性提升同时精度不降、耗时降 96%。</p>
</dd>
</dl>
